# 第 8 章　鸽群中的猫

*2.1 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Cat Among Pigeons　·　鸽群中的猫

## 060　The Devil in Velvet　天鹅绒里的恶魔

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：The Reverie (Dreamscape)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Aventurine; Black Swan; March 7th; Welt; Himeko; Dan Heng; Acheron; Firefly; Duke Inferno　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Whodunit'' completed

### Talk to Aventurine

> *You have witnessed the truth that can "result in the collapse of Penacony" as mentioned by Aventurine... Robin's body starts to disintegrate from the crack in her chest and is eventually reduced to nothingness in the dream. Aventurine seems to have something to say. Let's see what he has got up his sleeves.*

[Recap] Aventurine showed you the scene where Robin was killed, believing that only Acheron, an Emanator, had the ability to create "Death" in a dreamscape. He hoped that you would keep your distance from her, just to be safe.

*(Talk to Aventurine)*

**Aventurine:** ...Ah, friend. My expression wasn't much better than yours when I first saw this.

> **砂金：**…哎呀，朋友，我目睹这场面的时候，表情比你好不了多少。

**Aventurine:** Your eyes aren't deceiving you — it's her, the famous singer Robin.

> **砂金：**你没看错，就是她，那位声名显赫的歌者「知更鸟」。

- ▸ But... how is that possible?
- ▸ Could Robin actually...

**Aventurine:** First of all, can I just say that this had nothing to do with me. I'm just an unlucky bystander here. The Family can testify for me. If you don't believe me, just ask anyone in the Bloodhound Family — they hate me, and they hate the IPC, so they'd never lie.

> **砂金：**先向你声明，这事跟我无关，我只是个不幸撞进现场的倒霉蛋。家族可以作证，不信的话就找个猎犬家系的人打听打听吧，他们恨我，恨公司，所以绝不会说谎。

**Aventurine:** This is not where the crime happened. What I showed to you was a "memory" — the most basic Light Cone manifesting tech, authorized by the Garden of Recollection and owned by the IPC.

> **砂金：**这里也不是案发地，我为你展现的是一段「记忆」——最简单的光锥呈现技术，忆庭授权，公司所有。

**Aventurine:** Did you really think the Galaxy Ranger was an outsider this whole time?

> **砂金：**事到如今，你还觉得那位「巡海游侠」是局外人么？

**Aventurine:** Penacony has made a solemn commitment to protect the safety of anyone inside a Family dream. Any person in distress will be forcibly awakened and safely returned to reality.

> **砂金：**匹诺康尼可是郑重承诺过：在家族编织的美梦中，每一位客人的安全都会得到保障。遇险者会被强制唤醒，平安地回到现实。

**Aventurine:** What gives them the confidence to make such conclusive statements? Because behind this promise is the Harmony: The Family's Dreamweavers link up their minds together to construct an unbreakable defensive line.

> **砂金：**他们有什么底气这样言之凿凿？因为这承诺的背后是「同谐」的庇佑：家族的筑梦师们将思想连缀成一，构建起坚不可破的安全防线。

**Aventurine:** Breaking through this line of defense to create "Death" in the dreamscape... Not even a Memokeeper could do that without The Family's permission.

> **砂金：**突破这道防线，在梦境中创造「死亡」…未经家族许可，就连忆者都做不到这事。

**Aventurine:** Who could have done it? Friend, the only one is her, the girl who calls herself a Galaxy Ranger... An imposter, an unsought guest, an Emanator who hides her true identity...

> **砂金：**谁能做到？朋友，只能是她，那个自称巡海游侠的女人…一位冒名顶替的不速之客，隐瞒了真实身份的「令使」……

**Aventurine:** Ifrit's death was a foregone conclusion, and Robin... her misfortune was staring right at her. Who will be the next to die?

> **砂金：**阿弗利特的死已成定局，而知更鸟…她的惨状就在眼前。下一位牺牲者会是谁？

- ▸ This really is... hard to take in.
- ▸ That's just one side of the story.
- ▸ If that's the case, I can't trust anyone.

**Aventurine:** It's fine. Listen to your gut. Building trust always takes time, and I'm willing to wait.

> **砂金：**没事，你可以有自己的判断。培养信任总是需要时间，我愿意等待。

**Aventurine:** I just hope you realize that wherever that legacy is concerned... covert plans are already underway throughout Penacony. Everyone's got their own agenda.

> **砂金：**只是希望你能明白，围绕那份所谓的「遗产」…匹诺康尼暗流涌动，人们个个心怀鬼胎。

**Aventurine:** Careful you don't get stuck on the wrong side. If I were you, I'd keep my distance from Acheron. After all, any schemes out in the open are always going to be better than a monster in the shadows... right?

> **砂金：**千万别站错了边。我要是你，就会和黄泉保持距离。毕竟再怎么说，摆上台前的算计，也远胜过藏匿于阴影中的怪物…不是么？

**Black Swan:** Who's to say there isn't an even deeper conspiracy lurking beneath the surface?

> **黑天鹅：**但谁说明面的算计背后，没有更深的阴谋呢？

**Aventurine:** ...Memokeeper, I think our little deal is finished.

> **砂金：**…忆者，我想我们的交易早就结束了。

[Recap] Black Swan confirmed the authenticity of that segment of memory, and Aventurine asked to personally collaborate with the Astral Express. He had no intentions of fighting for the Legacy, and merely wanted to complete his job of reclaiming Penacony for the IPC.

**Black Swan:** Aventurine is telling the truth. This "memory" is a real one, and there's no sign of any distortion grafting on.

> **黑天鹅：**砂金说的是实话，这段「记忆」是真实的，没有任何歪曲嫁接的部分。

**Aventurine:** The IPC is not the Garden, and there are real limits to what they can actually do. But you know all this.

> **砂金：**公司不是忆庭，能做的事相当有限——这点你再清楚不过。

**Aventurine:** Friend, let's not beat around the bush here — the thing is, I want to reach out personally to team up with the Astral Express.

> **砂金：**朋友，不绕弯子了——开门见山地说，我想以个人名义同星穹列车达成合作。

**Aventurine:** I told you, I'm just not interested in scrambling for "the Legacy." I just came to Penacony for work. I'm here to retrieve some lost property for the IPC, if you catch my drift... I'm talking ownership of this "frontier prison."

> **砂金：**早和你说过，我对遗产争夺战没兴趣，来匹诺康尼只是出于工作。我要替公司收复一些失落的财产，你懂的…就是「边陲监狱」的所有权。

**Aventurine:** This has all become a bad debt thanks to the Cancer of All Worlds. The IPC has tried sitting down for negotiations time and again, but The Family wouldn't even take our calls.

> **砂金：**拜万界之癌所赐，这东西早成了一笔坏账，公司几次想坐下来谈谈，可家族连门都不给开。

**Aventurine:** You have no idea how difficult these people are to deal with. Put it this way — they've hushed up the existence of "Death" before, so they can definitely cover up any news about Robin's death.

> **砂金：**你不知道这帮人有多难对付。这么说吧，他们以前能瞒下「死亡」的存在，这次也一定能把知更鸟的死讯掩盖过去。

**Aventurine:** It'll just quietly float off like a bubble and pop, nobody ever being the wiser...

> **砂金：**就这么悄无声息地化作泡沫消散，到最后也无人知晓……

**Aventurine:** That's not fair, right? So then, friend, I need your help.

> **砂金：**这不公平，对不对？所以朋友，我需要你的帮助。

- ▸ What kind of help?
- ▸ I've got something on right now.

**Aventurine:** That's fine. I'm not forcing you.

> **砂金：**没关系，我不会强迫你。

**Aventurine:** But I'm really not asking for much, so why don't you at least hear me out, okay?

> **砂金：**但我想要的并不多，不妨先听我说完吧，可以么？

- ▸ I can't trust you.

**Aventurine:** That's fine. I'm not forcing you.

> **砂金：**没关系，我不会强迫你。

**Aventurine:** But I'm really not asking for much, so why don't you at least hear me out, okay?

> **砂金：**但我想要的并不多，不妨先听我说完吧，可以么？

**Aventurine:** I have but only one goal: The Family's front door is like a high wall, and to tear the whole thing down I'll have to dig out a few chunks first. Once I find a weak point, the IPC will have plenty of means.

> **砂金：**我只有一个目标：家族的大门是堵高墙，要推倒它，我得先凿几个洞出来。一旦出现破绽，公司就有的是手段。

**Aventurine:** Now we have our chance. So long as we can get to the truth behind her death, we can have justice for Robin while also gaining a valuable bargaining chip for bringing The Family to the table.

> **砂金：**现在，机会来了。只要我们能弄清「知更鸟之死」的真相，就能还死者一个公道，同时还能收获谈判的筹码，请家族上桌。

**Aventurine:** Truly a once-in-a-blue-moon opportunity... I've been investigating and making lots of friends all over Penacony precisely for this very moment.

> **砂金：**千载难逢的机会…我在匹诺康尼四处调查、广交朋友，为的就是这一刻。

**Aventurine:** This tragic news would be extremely bad for The Family, so they'll be doing everything they can to stop it leaking — especially to the IPC.

> **砂金：**这个噩耗对家族极其不利，所以他们一定会不遗余力地阻挠外人介入，尤其是公司。

**Aventurine:** But I trust there are still a few factions that remain exceptions, and that's why I need you all. The reputation of the Astral Express precedes you, and the Harmony will give you the fairest of appraisals.

> **砂金：**但我相信也有少数派系是例外，这就是我需要各位的理由。星穹列车名声在外，「同谐」也会给予你们最公正的评价。

**Aventurine:** You get to find out really what happened and seek justice, and I get to put it toward completing my mission for the IPC... It's what you call a win-win situation.

> **砂金：**你们能查明真相、伸张正义，而我能借此完成公司的任务…这就叫双赢。

- ▸ You want to take advantage of The Family's trust in us?
- ▸ The Nameless would never associate with the IPC.
- ▸ A decision like this is way above my pay grade...

**Aventurine:** Don't worry. Just head back and talk things over with your companions.

> **砂金：**别急嘛，你不妨先回到同伴们身边，和他们聊聊。

**Aventurine:** That navigator is really smart. She must understand the value of this deal. Look, here's my contact details. If you come to any conclusions, call me.

> **砂金：**那位领航员小姐是个聪明人，她一定明白这笔交易的价值。喏，我的联系方式，有结论了就联系我。

**Aventurine:** Oh, and... take this! A thorough investigation can always use a little more funding. Don't mention it!

> **砂金：**喔还有…拿去吧！要开展调查，手里总得有点闲钱。千万别客气！

**Aventurine:** So long, friend. I really am looking forward to uncovering the truth about "Death" with everyone.

> **砂金：**回见，朋友。我真的非常期待，能和各位一同揭开「死亡」的真相。

**(Trailblazer):** (Aventurine just sauntered off... He really doesn't mean to force it, but something still seems off...)

> **开拓者：**（砂金自顾自走了…他确实没有强迫的意思，但总感觉不对劲……）

**Black Swan:** ...What now? What are your plans?

> **黑天鹅：**…接下来呢？你打算怎么做？

**(Trailblazer):** (Black Swan... What is she thinking?)

> **开拓者：**（黑天鹅…她又是怎么想的？）

*(Obtain Credit)*

### Speak with Black Swan

> *Using Robin's death as an excuse, Aventurine invites you to collaborate with him in bringing down The Family. It is not coercion, but a temptation with the promise of gain. Although he leaves you with enough room to decide, you know that one should not be hasty when it comes to decision-making. You still have the chance to talk to Black Swan and ask for her advice before leaving the Memory Zone.*

[Recap] Black Swan reminded you that Aventurine was very intelligent, and that you should remain wary while cooperating with him. After summarizing the current situation with you, she suggested that you discussed this with your companions in detail, and sent you away.

*(Talk to Black Swan)*

**Black Swan:** On the surface, this doesn't look like a bad deal for you. But Aventurine is a shrewd merchant whose scheme won't just be as simple as it appears to be.

> **黑天鹅：**表面上看，这场交易对你没有坏处。但砂金是个精明的商人，算盘绝不会只有表面那么简单。

**Black Swan:** He doesn't know about Miss Firefly yet — but judging by your reaction, he may have noticed something going on and deliberately shifted topics to the "Truth of Death" to try and pull you in line with his way of thinking.

> **黑天鹅：**他还不知道流萤小姐的事——但从你的反应中，他或许有所察觉，就有意将话题诱导向「死亡的真相」，把你绕进他的逻辑里。

**Black Swan:** That's quick thinking, and very sound logic. Aventurine is no fool. And working with him definitely has its dangers.

> **黑天鹅：**反应迅速，逻辑清晰…我必须提醒你，和那个男人合作很危险。

- ▸ The Nameless cannot turn a blind eye to evil.

**Black Swan:** But you're talking about REAL evil.

> **黑天鹅：**前提是…真正的「邪恶」。

- ▸ For Firefly's sake, we must get to the bottom of all this.

**Black Swan:** That... is something I agree on.

> **黑天鹅：**这一点…我也赞同。

- ▸ Playing it safe seems to be the smarter move.

**Black Swan:** Insofar as things are presently concerned, yes. But what about the long-term?

> **黑天鹅：**就眼下的状况而言，确实。但长远来看呢？

- ▸ Can I toss a die to let it decide for me?

**Black Swan:** Leaving it up to fate? It's just how that gambler thinks.

> **黑天鹅：**交给命运吗？你的想法倒是与那位赌徒有几分相似了。

**Black Swan:** Anyway, be careful out there. There's more than one way to blaze a trail. In a dark forest beset by wolves ensuring your own escape to safety should be your primary concern.

> **黑天鹅：**无论如何，谨慎些吧。「开拓」的道路不止一条，在群狼环伺的黑暗森林中，你首先要确保自己能够逃出生天。

**Black Swan:** As for the other questions...

> **黑天鹅：**至于其他问题……

- ▸ Did "Death" kill Robin?

**Black Swan:** I'm not sure the two cases were committed by the same culprit, but that massive wound... looked like its winged blade. We've all witnessed it in action before.

> **黑天鹅：**我不敢肯定两起事件是同一人所为，但那道巨大的伤口…很像它的翼刃，我们都见过它行凶的瞬间。

**Black Swan:** Plus... it seems unlikely that there would be two lethal entities loose in the dreamscape.

> **黑天鹅：**而且…在梦境中致人死地的可能，目前没有第二种。

- ▸ Do you think this is connected to Acheron?

**Black Swan:** Sorry, I can't answer that question. That ranger is shrouded in mystery. I'm afraid no one is capable of providing an answer.

> **黑天鹅：**抱歉，我无法回答这个问题。那位游侠太过神秘，恐怕没人能给出定论。

**Black Swan:** But without a doubt... she is the most "special" guest at this banquet. It's like Aventurine said just then. It's best to keep your distance from her.

> **黑天鹅：**但毫无疑问…她是这场宴会中最「特别」的客人。正如砂金方才所说，你最好与她保持距离。

- ▸ Have you noticed anything else unusual?

**Black Swan:** Two victims appearing one after the other in a very short time span... In and of itself, that's very unusual.

> **黑天鹅：**在极短的时间内先后出现了两位牺牲者…这件事本身就过于异常。

**Black Swan:** Two possibilities: The collapse of Penacony's Dreamscape has started speeding up, making "Death" extremely agitated and weakening The Family's protections. Or, everything has been planned out and executed by someone.

> **黑天鹅：**有两种可能：匹诺康尼的梦境崩溃开始加速了，这让「死亡」变得异常躁动，家族的庇护也开始脆弱；又或者，所有的行动都在某个人的计划之中。

**Black Swan:** If someone has chosen these victims deliberately... First a stowaway, then a Family celebrity... Then this murderer's motives are worth thoroughly chewing over.

> **黑天鹅：**若是有人刻意而为…先是*偷渡犯*，后是*家族的明星*，凶手的用意难免有些耐人寻味。

- ▸ I don't have any other questions.

**Black Swan:** It's all happened so quickly I can only make conjecture. After leaving here, go have a chat with your companions. I hope you can clarify the source of this confusion.

> **黑天鹅：**这一切太突然，我也只能给出猜测。离开这里后，去和你的同伴仔细聊聊吧，希望你们能厘清纷乱思绪的源头。

Black Swan leads you through the chaotic Dreamscape...

### Follow Black Swan out of the hotel

> *Black Swan answers your questions and promises to escort you to the exit of the Memory Zone. Hopefully, she isn't up to any mischief this time... Or, do you secretly enjoy the little tricks she pulls?*

**Black Swan:** Come, this way — it's a short walk. Don't get lost.

> **黑天鹅：**来，这边——一小段路，别走丢了。

[Recap] Black Swan bade you farewell and gave you a card, a compensation for hiding things from you before. If you ever fell into the depths of the Memory Zone, this card would show you the way out.

*(Approach Black Swan)*

**Black Swan:** This way... This is where we part ways. All of this is like... a nightmare.

> **黑天鹅：**从这边离开吧。然后我们就该告别了。这一切就像是…一场噩梦。

**Black Swan:** Unfortunately, the Remembrance doesn't lie... What we just saw is the "reality" that happened — and it won't fade from our minds just because we wake up.

> **黑天鹅：**很不幸，记忆不会说谎…我们方才所见，都是真实发生的「现实」，不会因为醒来便消散在脑后。

**Black Swan:** But follow your heart and don't be afraid. We all walk through this world casting shadows of different lengths, and ultimately... all we leave behind are precious memories.

> **黑天鹅：**但不要害怕，听从内心的选择吧。我们都是这样行过世间，留下长短不一的影子，最后…留下珍贵的回忆。

**Black Swan:** ...Ah, hold on just a sec.

> **黑天鹅：**…啊，请再等一下。

Black Swan gently touches the root of your ear, leaving a cool sensation. Then, she hands a card over to you.

**Black Swan:** There you go, a small parting gift. If one day, you unfortunately fall into the deep waters of the Memory Zone and there's no Memokeeper to join you — hopefully it can guide you on my behalf.

> **黑天鹅：**这样就行了，一件小小的临别礼物。倘若有一日，你不幸沉沦于忆域的深海，又没有忆者相伴——它会代我为你指引出路。

**Black Swan:** I also pay great attention to the ways of the world... Just think of this as an apology from me for hiding something from you.

> **黑天鹅：**我也是很注重人情世故的…就把它当作我对你有所隐瞒的赔礼吧。

- ▸ Thank you.
- ▸ That's it?

**Black Swan:** Then... I've something private to take care of regarding that Galaxy Ranger. Let's leave things there, shall we?

> **黑天鹅：**接下来…我有些私事要处理，是关于那位巡海游侠的。我们就此别过吧。

**Black Swan:** What fascinating memories will you bring for me next time we meet...? I sincerely look forward to them.

> **黑天鹅：**下次见面时，你又会带来怎样有趣的记忆呢…我很期待哦。

### Wait for the Express Crew near the Clockie Statue

> *Safe and sound, you return to the Golden Hour with a repertoire of information. If everything goes as planned, the Crew will surely be blown away after hearing your experiences... provided that you don't get tongue-tied. Let's look forward to it.*

**(Trailblazer):** (A Family rep... Is Himeko okay...)

> **开拓者：**（家族代表…姬子那边没事吧……）

*(Approach Clockie Statue)*

**(Trailblazer):** (So much has happened... I should take a moment to gather my thoughts, and wait for everyone to arrive...)

### Watch the memory

> *Aren't you curious about what took place on the other side of Penacony while you were waiting for the Crew? Now you have a chance to see it — This can't be explained by science, so just enjoy it.*

Some time ago — Deep in Memory Zone

> **黄泉：**我已经在这么做了。

**Acheron:** "The so-called impossible is merely something that has yet to happen"...

> **黄泉：**「所谓的不可能之事，只是尚未到来之事」……

**Acheron:** Is there anything else in your "script" about me? I'd like to know what kind of footnote "I" get to leave in that future foreseen by destiny?

> **黄泉：**你的「剧本」中有任何关于我的部分吗？我想知道，在命运所见的未来中，「我」留下了怎样的注脚？

**Sam:** Unfortunately, not a thing came up.

> **萨姆：**很遗憾，只字未提。

**Acheron:** ...I knew it.

> **黄泉：**…并不意外。

**Sam:** Hang on...

> **萨姆：**等等——

**Sam:** I don't.

> **萨姆：**我不会。

> **黄泉：**什么？

> **萨姆：**你最初的提问——「你是否还会做梦，梦见那些因你而死的人。」

**Sam:** I don't. Never have.

> **萨姆：**我不会。从来不会。

**Sam:** So, I really envy you.

> **萨姆：**所以，我很羡慕你。

> **黄泉：**是么……

**Acheron:** Then you're already living in the waking world.

> **黄泉：**那你已经活在清醒的世界中了。

## 061　Lost Generation　迷惘的一代人

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Golden Hour　|　角色：Trailblazer; Aventurine; Himeko; March 7th; Welt; Acheron; Black Swan; The Dahlia; Dubra; Akash; Caterina; Duke Inferno　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Devil in Velvet'' completed

### Talk to the Crew

> *The Crew safely arrives at the Golden Hour and exchanges info with you. After an intense discussion, you decide to work with Aventurine. Before planning the next step, take some time to discuss what everyone is concerned about.*

[Recap] You discussed the two murders with the Crew and learned that The Family wanted your help to investigate those killings. After some negotiation, all of you decided to simultaneously cooperate with The Family and Aventurine to find the murderer.

Present day — Golden Hour

**March 7th:** We heard about Miss Firefly from Black Swan... But we never expected Miss Robin to...

**Himeko:** I'm sorry... that I couldn't be with you then.

**Welt:** Reality cruises on in serenity, while undercurrents bubble up from the Dreamscape... just like that Memokeeper said...

**Welt:** ...Stay strong, everyone. We can still do what we can for them... starting with finding the murderer.

**Himeko:** Let's recap everything then. (Trailblazer) just reminded me of something: March, do you remember what that Family rep who negotiated with us said?

**March 7th:** "Indeed, we trust that the Nameless has nothing to do with this, and we also beg each of you to help assist The Family in verifying the identity of the deceased." That's how it was put... in reference to Miss Firefly.

**Himeko:** Looking back, he seemed a little evasive at the time. And he also failed to mention anything about the earlier murder, too.

**March 7th:** The Family is planning on covering up all news about Miss Robin's death. If news gets out, Penacony is going to turn into a bloodbath...

**Welt:** But the murder that followed closely after was obviously beyond their anticipation. The Family had to try and turn things to their advantage by bringing in reinforcements from outside. The Charmony Festival is nearly here — they must be snowed under.

**Himeko:** It may also be that Miss Firefly's murder had so many witnesses that it couldn't be covered up, so they went with the flow and let more people on the scene to control the situation.

**Himeko:** After all, the nature of the two murders is fundamentally different... The Family's first protective measures should be against malicious actors among the guests, such as that IPC envoy.

- ▸ He really is up to no good.

**Welt:** All the while accusing that Galaxy Ranger...

- ▸ Aventurine is on guard against Acheron.

**Welt:** Indeed, he was particularly concerned about that Galaxy Ranger.

**March 7th:** Are we missing the forest for the trees here...? I always felt that Aventurine's reasons for accusing Miss Acheron were highly subtle. Can we believe him?

**Himeko:** At this point, I'm afraid the only ones we can trust are ourselves.

**Welt:** Look, let's try to gather intel first and then list all the possible outcomes we can. Then we go through them, eliminating contradictions one-by-one. The fewer facts remaining, the closer we are to the truth.

**Welt:** ...I've still got this sense of foreboding. It's like we're stuck in a whirlpool — spinning around that Legacy even after everything that's happened...

**March 7th:** This time we're playing the role of a real detective... But before we start, what are we going to say to The Family and Aventurine?

**Himeko:** As I see things, The Family harbors no ill-will towards the Astral Express — if they didn't trust the Crew, they wouldn't have casually commissioned outsiders to investigate a case that's in all likelihood "a scandal."

**Himeko:** Plus, this is The Family's turf. Teaming up with them should make things easier for us in the future.

**Himeko:** As for that Aventurine... Welt, I'd like to hear your thoughts.

**Welt:** He's complex. He deliberately slow-played his hand during negotiations while running circles round us all the while.

**Welt:** He appealed convincingly to both reason and emotion. It wasn't forced, but the intent was obvious.

**Welt:** Still, it's good to have contacts among all this uncertainty. Aventurine showed his skills, and as far as our interests are aligned, he can become a reliable ally.

**Welt:** We also need to keep a certain distance from The Family — never let them get too close. Teaming up with the IPC helps balance that out — if either side makes a move, we have the option to pull out.

**Himeko:** So you suggest accepting Aventurine's proposal to team up?

**Welt:** Yes... It's risky, but we can only wait until both sides have played their cards before making any further judgments.

**March 7th:** I get why, but there's a whole lot of bad guys and girls around here and I'm worried about getting stabbed in the back. (Trailblazer) has been bullied a few times now, and I can't stand it anymore...

- ▸ It's fine. Let's focus on the big picture for now.

**March 7th:** You're like a broken record...

- ▸ It's fine. I got a lot out of him, too...

**March 7th:** You...

**March 7th:** ...No! I can't accept this!

- ▸ It's cool. You can bully me whenever!

**March 7th:** You...

**March 7th:** ...No! I can't accept this!

**March 7th:** ...Forget about it. Just let me keep an eye on him! If that doesn't work, we can just turn the tables and use him instead!

**Himeko:** Then (Trailblazer), could you please reply to Aventurine? Everyone, take this time to put together your thoughts.

*(Open chat with Aventurine)*

### Talk to Himeko

[Recap] You spoke to Himeko about Aventurine's motives. He came to reclaim Penacony for the IPC, and wanted to investigate "Death" to find an advantage that could overthrow The Family. Thus, he was willing to bet everything for this victory.

*(Talk to Himeko)*

**Himeko:** Aventurine's goal is to try and recapture Penacony for IPC. To do this, he'll have to bring down The Family in its entirety to create a big enough chance.

> **姬子：**砂金的目标是为公司收复匹诺康尼。为此，他必须扳倒家族，制造出足够大的破绽。

**Himeko:** The existence of "Death" will be covered up by The Family, so how does he plan on taking them down? It's got to be something important enough that everyone will notice, but it also can't be anything too out-in-the-open...

> **姬子：**「死亡」的存在会被家族掩盖，他打算从什么地方破局？切入点必须足够重要，所有人都会关注，但过程中又不能太张扬……

- ▸ Perhaps he's going for the Hotel?

**Himeko:** An attack on the hotel guests? Unlikely. Penacony's guests include quite a few big wigs known throughout the whole cosmos, people who not even the IPC would dare take lightly. Aventurine is a shrewd merchant, and there's no way he doesn't know that.

> **姬子：**对住客下手吗？不像。匹诺康尼的客人有不少寰宇皆知的大人物，即便是公司也不敢轻易动这些人。砂金是个精明的商人，不可能不明白这点。

- ▸ Perhaps he's going for The Family?

**Himeko:** He's definitely going for The Family, and it's just a matter of how. The Harmony is strong in Penacony and almost impossible to take on head-to-head. The fact that the IPC dispatched Aventurine shows that they do not intend to simply play by the book here.

> **姬子：**他的目标一定是家族，但方法不得而知。「同谐」在匹诺康尼势力壮大，想正面与之抗衡非常困难。公司指派砂金前来，也说明了他们不打算依靠正常手段。

- ▸ Perhaps he's going for Acheron?

**Himeko:** Aventurine has devoted considerable attention to her. But this Galaxy Ranger... we know hardly anything about her and can't rush to any conclusions.

> **姬子：**砂金对她投入了相当多的关注。但对这位巡海游侠…我们目前知之甚少，做不出什么判断。

- ▸ Perhaps he's going for the Astral Express?

**Himeko:** ...I was also considering this possibility, especially... because he respects you so much, and has sought you out before a few times. Perhaps he's also unsure of your intentions, and is probing you.

> **姬子：**…我也在考虑这个可能性，尤其是…他对你格外看重，屡次刻意接近。或许他也不清楚你的虚实，还在反复试探。

- ▸ No conclusions can be drawn just yet.

**Himeko:** ...I'm just speculating. In any case, we have to be careful when handling Aventurine. He's skilled at reading people and discerning the right moment to strike. Also...

> **姬子：**…现在也就是随便想想。无论如何，和砂金打交道都得保持警惕，他很擅长把握人心，懂得什么时候该出手。并且……

**Himeko:** He's clearly a born gambler if he's willing to go all in to win.

> **姬子：**他显然是个天生的赌徒，愿意为胜利押上所有。

*(Talk to Himeko again, optional)*

**Himeko:** If Aventurine wants to bring down The Family, he'll have to create a big enough chance. After everything he's done so far — what is his goal...?

> **姬子：**砂金想要扳倒家族，就必须制造出足够大的破绽。他至今为止的行动…目的究竟是什么……

### Talk to Welt

> **瓦尔特：**在没有任何证据的情况下，他直接指控那位巡海游侠是杀死知更鸟的嫌犯。可对于她和「忆域迷因」的联系，以及为何要潜伏在你身边，却只字未提。

> **瓦尔特：**不过，前来赴约的泯灭帮失去音讯一事，我向留守列车的丹恒求证了…不是砂金凭空编造。

- ▸ ...She's a very mysterious woman?
- ▸ Weird... I can't seem to remember.
- ▸ ...She's a very powerful woman?
- ▸ Weird... I can't seem to remember.
- ▸ Weird... I can't seem to remember.

### Talk to March 7th

[Recap] You spoke with March 7th. She felt that everything that happened in the dreamscape did not feel realistic. Usually, only joyful events happen in a sweet dream. You consoled her and said that you would let the sweet dream return to normal.

**March 7th:** I hope it's not too soon to bring it up, but I feel like... Miss Robin isn't actually dead, but that she's still alive and well somewhere. That everything's just some... horrible prank...

> **三月七：**不知道是不是太突然了，我总觉得…知更鸟小姐没有死，还好好地活在什么地方。一切就只是场…恶作剧……

**March 7th:** Because... aren't we supposed to be inside a dream? How could someone die in a beautiful dreamscape like this... Shouldn't only good things happen here?

> **三月七：**因为…这不是在梦里吗？美梦乐园怎么会有人死掉呢，不应该只有好事发生…才对吗？

> **三月七：**唉，看到那座大剧院，就会忍不住胡思乱想……

> **三月七：**街上的人们依旧好开心，没人知道发生了什么…好不真实，就好像流萤和知更鸟小姐，还有我们…是另一个世界的人。

> **三月七：**但这不就跟街上的人一样了吗…唔……

**Aventurine:** One of the fragments flew upstairs. I'll need to use the pinball machine to flick myself up there, but it's tough.

[Recap] Aventurine walked up to the Pinball Machine model and noticed that its base was empty. Dr. Ratio searched for a usable pinball model for him in the hall.

*(Investigate base)*

**Aventurine:** Great. There's another pinball machine base here, and it's empty too.

> **砂金：**没有那个弹珠机模型——我们都得交待在这儿——！

**Aventurine:** ...Doc, I'll need your brainpower again.

> **砂金：**诶，拉帝奥——，我听到了——

**Dr. Ratio:** There's no need to yell, I can hear you. The pinball machine must be hidden somewhere in the hall, like the arch. Wait here, and I'll be back in a minute.

> **真理医生：**就是它了…愉快的独处时光总是转瞬即逝。

### Head to the Meditation Room and look for the pinball machine model

> *You ask the knowledgeable Dr. Ratio to help you look for a pinball machine model in the Meditation Room. So happy for him!*

**Dr. Ratio:** Finally, a moment of peace.

*(Investigate sandpit, optional)*

**Dr. Ratio:** Quite a fascinating contraption. I would rate it more favorably if that clamorous little person inside were gone.

**Aventurine:** Ratio... I can hear you...

*(Stay idle at north side of sandpit)*

**Aventurine:** Doctor... Shake a leg...?

**Aventurine:** Without that Pinball machine, neither one of us is going anywhere anytime soon.

> **砂金：**哈，你回来了——！把它放在这儿就行——感激不尽——！

**Dr. Ratio:** ...

*(Investigate model)*

**Dr. Ratio:** That's it... Pleasant moments of solitude are always fleeting.

*(Obtain Pinball Machine Model)*

### Place the pinball machine model on the sandpit

> *You ask the knowledgeable Dr. Ratio to help you look for a pinball machine model in the Meditation Room, and he manages to find it. So happy for him!*

*(Approach sandpit)*

**Aventurine:** Ha, you're back! Just place it here... Thank you...!

> **砂金：**全部搞定，轻轻松松。

[Recap] Dr. Ratio set the Pinball Machine model on its foundation in the miniature sandpit.

*(Cutscene plays)*

### Head to higher ground to collect Jigsaw Fragments

> *Dream Jigsaws are ubiquitous in Penacony. Generally speaking, seeing a Dream Jigsaw is the same as seeing a door... Intentional or not, this creepy design is so common in Penacony that it makes you wonder whether The Family has run out of creative ideas. Anyway, you might as well go through the procedure and collect all the jigsaw fragments. You know how your professor friend isn't known for his patience.*

**Aventurine:** Thanks, Doc!

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Aventurine:** Ratio, you should come in and take a look. The view here is breathtaking. Honestly, you could easily squash me with just a pinch.

**Dr. Ratio:** If that is your wish, I will do so without a moment's hesitation.

**Aventurine:** All done. Piece of cake.

### Complete the Dream Jigsaw

> *Dream Jigsaws are ubiquitous in Penacony. Generally speaking, seeing a Dream Jigsaw is the same as seeing a door... Intentional or not, this creepy design is so common in Penacony that it makes you wonder whether The Family has run out of creative ideas. Finally, you have collected all the fragments. It's high time to leave this desolate and eerie place.*

*(Solve Dream Jigsaw)*

**Aventurine:** And the joyous tour of Toy City has come to an end. It makes me feel sad.

> **砂金：**愉快的玩具城之旅结束了，还有点舍不得呢。

**Aventurine:** Penacony isn't all bad, right? I'll use this interesting experience as a talking point at the poker table.

> **砂金：**匹诺康尼也不都是坏事，对吧？我会把这段有趣经历当作牌桌上的谈资的。

### Head to The Family's council chamber and face Sunday

> *Now, you're all set to meet the head of Oak Family. Good luck.*

[Recap] Aventurine and Dr. Ratio arrived at the end of Dewlight Pavilion. Aventurine admitted that his accusation of Acheron was merely a cover, and the real murderer was within The Family. His plan was to coerce Sunday into cooperating, through "fear" and "benefits."

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Dr. Ratio:** It's a pity you made it out of the sandpit alive. Sunday is just beyond this door. From my limited understanding, he's not someone easily handled. Are you prepared?

> **真理医生：**很遗憾看到你活着离开沙盘，星期日就在这扇门后。以我粗浅的见解，他不是什么好对付的人，你准备好了么？

**Aventurine:** Yeah, only I believe he's the one who should be prepared to face me.

> **砂金：**嗯，我更相信要做好准备的是他。

**Dr. Ratio:** Tell me about your plan.

> **真理医生：**说说你的计划吧。

**Aventurine:** I don't have a plan. I'll just play it by ear. There are only two kinds of bargaining chips when dealing with people: Benefit or fear.

> **砂金：**没什么计划，随机应变。与人交涉的筹码无非两种：利益或者恐惧。

**Dr. Ratio:** Looks like "sincerity" isn't in your dictionary.

> **真理医生：**看来你的确不理解「真诚」。

**Aventurine:** Am I not sincere enough? There's no need to emphasize it. We've got to make good use of death. That man's sister is dead. He won't be able to turn a blind eye, and that's "fear."

> **砂金：**我还不够真诚么？不用特意强调。我们要好好利用死亡，那男人的妹妹死了，他肯定坐不住的，这就是「恐惧」。

**Aventurine:** And I'll help him find the murderer. He can't do it due to his status and position, but I can, and that's "benefit."

> **砂金：**而我会帮他把那个杀人凶手揪出来。碍于身份和立场，他自己办不到这事，但我可以，这就是「利益」。

**Dr. Ratio:** On what basis do you believe he's incapable, necessitating the delegation to someone from a rival faction, the IPC?

> **真理医生：**你凭什么觉得他做不到，非得委托一个立场对立的公司人？

**Aventurine:** Simple — Because that murderer could very well be a traitor hiding inside The Family.

> **砂金：**很简单——因为那凶手很可能是潜伏在家族中的*叛徒*。

**Dr. Ratio:** ...Do you mean the Galaxy Ranger whom you accused previously?

> **真理医生：**…你之前指控的可是那个巡海游侠。

**Aventurine:** That was just an excuse, good doctor. There's something wrong with that woman, and we need someone to keep her in check. It's better to minimize the variables outside of our control when we execute our plans.

> **砂金：**那就是个借口，教授。那女人不对劲，我需要有人牵制她，在我们行动时视野外的变数越少越好。

**Aventurine:** Moreover, I need to know her identity. If I'm lucky, she could be an important pawn. And it's good to have more helpful friends when dealing with this matter.

> **砂金：**我也需要知道她是什么人，如果我的好运货真价实，她一定能成为重要的棋子。而在这件事上能帮我的*朋友*，越多越好。

**Aventurine:** But honestly, the murder case is likely unrelated to her. I believe my standpoint: There's a rat in The Family. Otherwise, why would Mr. Sunday arrange a private meeting with us? This isn't an interrogation, but a secret negotiation.

> **砂金：**但说真心话，命案多半和她无关，我依旧是那个观点：肯定是家族内部出了问题。不然我们的星期日先生为何要安排私下会面？这不是一场审讯，而是一次秘密谈判。

**Aventurine:** We'll see. Using Robin's death as a bargaining chip, I'll win back my freedom and power. In the end, I'll ruin this beautiful dream and create the grandest death.

> **砂金：**看着吧，以知更鸟的死为筹码，我会为自己赢回自由和力量。最后，我会颠覆这场美梦，创造最盛大的死亡。

**Aventurine:** If the chance of winning is just beyond this door, even if that chance is close to zero, well... you can't win if you don't play, right?

> **砂金：**如果踏进这扇门就能迎来凯旋的机会，哪怕概率无限趋近于零，我也没有犹豫的理由，不是么？

**Dr. Ratio:** Ah, the charming audacity! To think that you, of all people, might emerge victorious, dear gambler.

> **真理医生：**你凭什么觉得自己能赢，该死的赌徒？

**Aventurine:** Three chips are enough.

> **砂金：**三枚「筹码」足矣。

**Aventurine:** All or nothing.

> **砂金：**所有，或者一无所有。

### Meet Sunday face-to-face

[Recap] Sunday accused Aventurine of intervening with The Family's internal matters, but the latter hinted that Sunday was restricted by his own identity, and could not pursue the real killer. Aventurine offered to investigate in his stead, but wanted a gift in return, the Cornerstone, and freedom.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Sunday:** It seems my puzzles are too effortless for you, IPC Ambassador.

> **星期日：**看来我布置的谜题对你还是太简单了，公司的使节。

**Aventurine:** I appreciate your words, and I see you put a lot of effort into welcoming me, Mr. Sunday. However, this is no way to greet a guest.

> **砂金：**承蒙谬赞，也感谢您花了这么多心思来欢迎我，星期日先生。只是这实在不像诚心邀约之人会做的事。

**Sunday:** Well, this isn't an invitation, but a summoning. Before we speak, I need to test your character.

> **星期日：**所以这并非邀请，而是传唤，在谈话开始前，我需要对你的品性做些考验。

**Sunday:** I imagine this knowledgeable doctor friend of yours has been of great help, yes?

> **星期日：**我猜，你身边这位博学的朋友帮了不少忙吧？

**Aventurine:** Certainly, you ought to know this better than I do — he has already faithfully fulfilled his duties, hasn't he?

> **砂金：**当然，您应该比我更清楚这点——他已经忠实地履行了自己的职责，对吧？

**Sunday:** Yes, the doctor has assured me of your noble character. He considers you, like himself, a virtuous person who can be trusted by The Family.

> **星期日：**嗯，此前教授为你高贵的人格做了保证。他说你们二人的心地一样正直，是家族可以信赖的对象。

**Sunday:** I have come to know you very well as a person, Mr. Aventurine. You're diligent, generous, and willing to cooperate. The fact that you succeeded in overcoming many obstacles just to meet me gave me the reason to believe in your wisdom and courage.

> **星期日：**我现在非常了解你的为人了，砂金先生。你勤勉、慷慨、乐于合作，又成功穿越重重阻碍来到我的面前——这令我有理由相信你的智慧与果敢。

**Sunday:** But there's one thing I must ask you — that is, you've used your wisdom at the wrong place to meet the wrong person and put yourself in a situation where you shouldn't be... witnessing a tragedy that shouldn't have happened.

> **星期日：**但有一件事，我是要质问你的，那就是你的才智偏偏用错了地方，令你约见不该约见的人，出现在不该出现的场合…目睹了不应发生的惨剧。

**Aventurine:** You don't look too well. Am I making you anxious?

> **砂金：**您的脸色看起来不太好啊。姑且确认一下，让您感到焦躁的是我吗？

**Aventurine:** ...If not, then it means I'm on your side.

> **砂金：**…如果不是，那我就是站在您这边的。

**Sunday:** If I wasn't mistaken, you'd just made a serious accusation against The Family.

> **星期日：**如果我没理解错，你这番话…可是对*家族*提出了极其严重的指控。

**Aventurine:** You weren't mistaken, for depravity is creeping in around you.

> **砂金：**您确实没理解错，因为邪恶正在您的身边悄然滋生。

**Aventurine:** There's no need for us to be evasive. Let's talk about your sister. Your sister's talent is unrivaled in the world of show business. As you know, her voice has been out of tune since she returned to Penacony. What's more disheartening, she can't sing anymore.

> **砂金：**我们不必遮遮掩掩，来谈谈您的妹妹吧。令妹的才能在演艺界无出其右，可您也知道，回到匹诺康尼后，她的声音就一直不太*谐调*。

**Aventurine:** Who could be responsible for this? Many suspect the culprit is among the outsiders, but I know... you hold a different opinion.

> **砂金：**更可怕的是，她现在再也无法歌唱了。谁做的？人们都觉得凶手在外来者中，但我知道…您心里另有答案。

**Aventurine:** Now, your noble status has become a shackle, preventing you from apprehending the murderer and avenging your sister's death. You're feeling anxious because you're out on a limb.

> **砂金：**如今，您高贵的身份反成了镣铐，让您无法出手缉拿凶手，为令妹报仇雪恨。您孤立无援，才会感到焦躁不堪。

**Aventurine:** But don't worry. I am on your side.

> **砂金：**但别担心，我是站在您这边的。

**Sunday:** I'm immensely honored by your concern for me, Mr. Aventurine — since you're so selfless and generous, I believe you wouldn't ask for anything in return, would you?

> **星期日：**砂金先生如此为我着想，是我莫大的荣幸——那么你这样无私慷慨的人，应该不会要求回报吧？

**Aventurine:** Naturally, you wouldn't incur any loss from this. I just want to reclaim what is mine: My liberty, and the personal items under The Family's custody — the bag of gift money, and...

> **砂金：**当然，您不会因此损失什么，我只想取回本属于自己的东西：人身自由，还有家族保管下的随身物品——那袋礼金，还有……

**Sunday:** The box in which the Cornerstone is stored.

> **星期日：**存放「基石」的匣子。

**Aventurine:** That's right.

> **砂金：**没错。

**Sunday:** Cornerstone — I've heard it's a treasured asset of the Strategic Investment Department, a sacred stone that seals the "Preservation" Emanator, granting significant power, and every Liquidation Specialist holds one.

> **星期日：**「基石」——我听闻那是战略投资部的宝贵资产，封存「存护」令使大权的圣石，列位清算专家各自持有一枚。

**Sunday:** ...For an object so precious, it probably comes at an even higher price than other forms of recompense.

> **星期日：**…如此贵重的物品，恐怕只比其他回报更为昂贵。

**Aventurine:** I'm sure you're aware of the high level of risk I'll be undertaking to bring the truth to light.

> **砂金：**但您也知道，若想真相水落石出，一点高昂的风险是必须的。

**Sunday:** Mr. Aventurine, when you're out and about, do you always make adjustments to your appearance? Your tie should be on the center line, your shirt must not protrude from your vest, your trouser creases should be perfectly straight, and always aligned with the tips of your shoes.

> **星期日：**砂金先生，出门在外，你会时刻关注自己的仪容么？领带应在正中线上，衬衣不得从马甲中露出，裤线必须笔直，且始终对齐鞋头的朝向。

**Aventurine:** Of course.

> **砂金：**当然会。

**Sunday:** But I don't, because it's not appropriate to do so in public — you should make sure everything is presentable and in order before leaving the house.

> **星期日：**但我不会，因为这不*得体*——你应当在出门前就确保一切井然有序，绝不偏移。

**Sunday:** I'm not the kind that takes risks. The Cornerstone must be in the custody of The Family.

> **星期日：**我从不承担任何风险。基石必须由家族来保管。

**Aventurine:** No room for negotiation?

> **砂金：**真没得聊？

**Sunday:** Please don't let me turn you down twice.

> **星期日：**别让我拒绝第二遍。

**Aventurine:** ...Sure. The gift money is good enough. I suppose you wouldn't mind that? After all, a merchant can't function without a bargaining chip.

> **砂金：**…行吧，只拿回礼金也可以，这你总该给我了。一个商人如果没有交易的筹码，恐怕寸步难行啊。

**Sunday:** You compromised quicker than I thought. Unfortunately, it's a gambler that needs a bargaining chip... not a merchant. I can give you your gift money, but before that, I want you to tell me —

> **星期日：**你的妥协比我预想中还要快些。可惜，比起商人…赌徒才更需要筹码。我可以给你礼金，但在这之前，我要你亲口告诉我——

[Recap] Sunday used the power of Harmony to interrogate Aventurine, such that he could not lie about the contents of the box in which the Cornerstone was stored. Aventurine insisted that the aventurine stone was safely in this box.

**Sunday:**— The fact that you can decisively forsake the box you asked for, what exactly is stored in it?

> **星期日：**——这个被你果断放弃的匣子里，究竟存放着什么？

**Sunday:** "Oh, Triple-Faced Soul, please sear his tongue and palms with a hot iron, so that he will not be able to fabricate lies and make false vows."

> **星期日：**「三重面相的灵魂啊，请你用热铁烙他的舌和手心，使他不能编造谎话，立定假誓。」

**Aventurine:** ...What have you done?

> **砂金：**…你做了什么？

**Sunday:** Under the light of the Harmony, all wickedness is revealed. I implore THEM to shed THEIR light, and I'll ask you questions on THEIR behalf. Next... you have 113 seconds to prove your innocence and gain my trust.

> **星期日：**「同谐」的光照下，一切罪恶无所遁形，我恳请祂降下光芒，并代祂向你提问。接下来…你有113秒的时间自证清白，得到我的信任。

**Aventurine:** And if I refuse to answer?

> **砂金：**如果我拒绝回答呢？

**Sunday:** You can try — and we'll see if the Harmony rejects you.

> **星期日：**那就试试看——看「同谐」会不会拒绝你。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Sunday:** "Question: Do you own a Cornerstone?"

> **星期日：**「试问：你是否持有基石？」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** What a simple answer. You, too, understand that idle chatter leads only to poverty.

> **星期日：**很简洁的回答。你也明白言多必失的道理。

**Sunday:** "Did you hand over the Cornerstone to The Family when you entered Penacony?"

> **星期日：**「你在入境时，是否将基石交予家族？」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** "Does the Cornerstone you handed over to The Family belong to you?"

> **星期日：**「你交予家族的基石是否属于你？」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** "Is your Cornerstone in this room right now?"

> **星期日：**「此刻，你的基石是否就在这个房间里？」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** "Is your memory free from any kind of tampering or deletion, encompassing but not restricted to the techniques of the Garden of Recollection?"

> **星期日：**「你的记忆是否没有遭到任何形式的篡改、删除，包括但不限于流光忆庭的技术。」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** "Are you an Avgin from Sigonia?"

> **星期日：**「你是否来自茨冈尼亚的埃维金氏族？」

**Aventurine:** Yes. You even know about that?

> **砂金：**是。你连这个都知道？

**Sunday:** "Do the Avgins have any ability to read, tamper with, or manipulate one's own or another's mind?"

> **星期日：**「埃维金人是否没有任何读取、篡改、操纵自己或他人思想的能力？」

**Aventurine:** No. Does it matter?

> **砂金：**没有，这有关系吗？

**Sunday:** "Do you love your family more than yourself?"

> **星期日：**「你爱家人胜过爱你自己吗？」

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**是。

**Sunday:** "All the Avgins were killed in a massacre. Am I right?"

> **星期日：**「所有埃维金人都在一场屠杀中丧命了，是吗？」

**Aventurine:** No.

> **砂金：**不是。

**Sunday:** "Are you your clan's sole survivor?"

> **星期日：**「你是氏族中唯一的幸存者吗？」

**Aventurine:** ...Perhaps.

> **砂金：**…也许吧。

**Sunday:** "...Do you hate and wish to destroy this world with your own hands?"

> **星期日：**「——你憎恨，并想要亲手毁灭这个世界吗？」

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** I don't know.

> **砂金：**我不知道。

**Sunday:** Interesting. Now, the final question...

> **星期日：**有趣。那么最后一个问题……

**Sunday:** "Can you swear that at this very moment, the Aventurine stone is safe and sound in this box?"

> **星期日：**「你是否能够立誓，此刻——『砂金石』正安然无恙地躺在这个匣子里？」

**Dr. Ratio:** ...

> **真理医生：**……

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** ...Of course.

> **砂金：**…当然。

**Sunday:** Looks like we can get an answer.

> **星期日：**看来我们能得出答案了。

**Sunday:** Open it, Mr. Aventurine... It's your last chance to defend your honor.

> **星期日：**打开它吧，砂金先生…这是你维护自己名誉的最后机会。

[Recap] Sunday gestured for Aventurine to open the box, which was supposed to contain the Cornerstones. Aventurine attempted to hide his distress while opening it, finding it empty. Sunday displayed two Cornerstones, showing that Aventurine's scheme had been uncovered. Dr. Ratio looked away from Aventurine, who understood that Dr. Ratio had turned against him.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Sunday:** Please.

> **星期日：**请。

**Sunday:** Are these what you're looking for?

> **星期日：**你在找的…是它们么？

*(Cutscene ends)*

Sunday and Dr. Ratio had a secret agreement to exchange information about the Stellaron for Aventurine's plan. Dr. Ratio claimed that the Cornerstone box contained the topaz stone, and the aventurine stone was in a gift bag.

System hours ago — Dewlight Pavilion

**Sunday:** Since you came as promised, learned Doctor... Does this mean that you are willing to take the side of The Family in this farce?

> **星期日：**既然您如期赴约，博学的教授…这是否意味着，您愿意在这场闹剧中站在家族这边？

**Dr. Ratio:** What makes you think you can convince me?

> **真理医生：**是什么让你觉得自己可以拉拢我？

**Sunday:** I've heard you haven't enjoyed Mr. Aventurine's company. I also understand that you're an avid learner who sees the pursuit of knowledge above all.

> **星期日：**我已有所耳闻，您与砂金先生的相处并不愉快。我也知道您是一位真正的学者，对知识的追求大过其他一切。

**Dr. Ratio:** In that case, you ought to realize that a competent scholar knows their position and wouldn't forsake more vital matters for the sake of petty pride.

> **真理医生：**那你应该也明白，一位合格的学者能认清自己的位置，不会为无聊的尊严丢失更重要的东西。

**Sunday:** If you agree to assist The Family, I'll share our research findings on the Stellaron. You must be quite aware that, besides The Family, no other faction is willing to share such information.

> **星期日：**若您同意协助家族，我会把我们对星核的研究成果如数奉上。您应该很清楚，除了家族，没有任何派系愿意分享这样的知识。

**Dr. Ratio:** ...

> **真理医生：**……

**Dr. Ratio:** Cut to the chase. What do you need from me?

> **真理医生：**说说看吧，需要我做什么？

**Sunday:** I need Mr. Aventurine's comprehensive plan.

> **星期日：**我需要砂金先生的全盘计划。

**Dr. Ratio:** Haven't you confiscated his Cornerstone? You can't expect a featherless bird to take flight.

> **真理医生：**你们不都把他的「基石」扣下了？拔光了毛的孔雀可飞不起来。

**Sunday:** But I've also heard the ten elites in the Strategic Investment Department have united, progressing together in the interests of the IPC.

> **星期日：**但我也听闻战略投资部的十位精英极为团结，在公司利益面前共同进退。

**Dr. Ratio:** You'll have to speak more clearly than that.

> **真理医生：**你可以把话说的更明白些。

**Sunday:** The Cornerstone which Mr. Aventurine surrendered — was it really his?

> **星期日：**砂金先生呈交的「基石」——当真属于他本人么？

**Dr. Ratio:** You question whether he would entrust you with someone else's Cornerstone? The Ten Stonehearts aren't as united as you think — Cornerstones are significantly more precious to them than their very own lives.

> **真理医生：**你怀疑他会把别人的基石交给你？你把石心十人想的太团结了——那玩意可比他们的命重要得多。

**Sunday:** But you know that he's a crazed gambler. The more vocal he is about it, the more cautious I must be.

> **星期日：**但您也知道他是个疯狂的赌徒。愈是声势张扬，愈要细心提防。

**Dr. Ratio:** I never imagined someone would share his way of thinking. Honestly, you should see a shrink.

> **真理医生：**我从没想过会有人和他思路一致。说真的，你最好去看看心理医生。

**Dr. Ratio:** Bring it. The box containing Cornerstone is unique, and only IPC senior staff and related members can access it — but I happen to be among them.

> **真理医生：**拿来吧。存放基石的匣子是特制的，除公司高层及相关人员外，没人能打开它——但我恰好位列其中。

> **星期日：**劳驾。

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

> **星期日：**呵，金黄色的石头啊。它的色泽和克里珀圣体的光芒如出一辙。

**Dr. Ratio:** This is the very ruse he intends to use to fool you. He won't reveal to you that the Ten Stonehearts chisel their own will into the Cornerstones, granting them an unparalleled radiance.

> **真理医生：**这正是他准备用来欺瞒你的说辞。他不会告诉你：石心十人需以自身意志开凿基石，令其绽放独一无二的光辉。

**Dr. Ratio:** And this golden stone is also known as a topaz, not aventurine, and it belongs to Topaz.

> **真理医生：**而这颗金黄色的石头属于托帕，它的别名是「黄玉」——不是「砂金」。

**Dr. Ratio:** So? Do you wish to confront him?

> **真理医生：**如何，要找他对质么？

**Sunday:** Not at the moment. I'm more interested to know the location of his Cornerstone.

> **星期日：**暂且不必。我现在更希望知道，属于他的那枚基石在哪。

**Dr. Ratio:** The safest place, somewhere you'd never think of. Because he never intended to hide it — in fact, that Cornerstone has been in your hands from the very beginning.

> **真理医生：**最安全的场所，你绝对想不到的地方。因为他根本就没打算藏起来——从最开始，那颗基石就已经在你的手里了。

**Sunday:** I see, this bag... Mixing a Cornerstone, more precious than life itself, with a bunch of worthless jewels, disguised as a gift of money waiting to be confiscated, is indeed in line with Mr. Aventurine's style.

> **星期日：**原来如此，这行李袋…将比性命更珍贵的基石混在一堆低贱的珠宝里，伪装成礼金等待被扣押，倒确实符合那位砂金先生的风格。

**Dr. Ratio:** Then he makes up some trivial excuse, downplaying the matter, and requests the gift money. This is a gamble, one he's all too familiar with, betting on your single misstep leading to a total loss.

> **真理医生：**然后再随便编个理由，避重就轻，找你把礼金要回来。这是场赌局，他可太熟悉了，赌的就是你一着不慎，满盘皆输。

**Sunday:** Learned Doctor, I'm grateful for your help. The Family will surely reward a righteous person like you.

> **星期日：**博学的教授，感谢您的帮助。家族自会答谢义人。

**Sunday:** As for the villain... I hope he retreats in humiliation.

> **星期日：**至于恶徒…愿他退后受辱。

Because Aventurine lied, he was sentenced to death by the Harmony and was only left with 17 system hours of freedom. Sunday returned the gift bag to him and ordered him to bring him the truth with what remains of his life.

**Sunday:** It was all thanks to your friend with a keen eye that I could add a blot of utter failure to your storied career.

> **星期日：**多亏你有一位眼光独到的朋友，我才能为你的职业生涯添上一次彻底的失败。

> **砂金：**拉帝奥，你这混蛋……

**Sunday:** Finally shown your true colors, heh. Oh, and just to remind you: You currently only have seventeen system hours left to live. Treasure your remaining time, and savor the delectable aftertaste of defeat.

> **星期日：**原形毕露了啊。顺便一提，你的生命*暂时*只剩下十七个系统时了。珍惜这段时间，好好回味失败的余韵吧。

**Aventurine:** ...You might as well explain yourself a little more clearly.

**Sunday:** What I performed on you just now was the Harmony's consecration. You were to show allegiance beneath the illumination of THEIR grace, yet you acted willfully, uttering nothing but falsehoods, transforming the consecration into a trial. I genuinely see no reason to absolve you from it.

> **星期日：**我方才为你施行的，是「同谐」的圣洗。你本应在祂的光照下展现忠诚，却一意孤行，满口谎言，将洗礼变作了审判。我实在没有理由为你解开它。

**Aventurine:** Is this what the Harmony represents? Is it built upon constraint and coercion?

> **砂金：**这就是所谓的「同谐」？…建立在拘禁和逼迫之上？

**Sunday:** You misunderstand, Mr. Aventurine. Punishment is meant for the irreverent, but I have seen your resilient spirit, and thus I offer you the possibility of a new beginning.

> **星期日：**你误会了，砂金先生。刑罚是为亵慢之人准备的，但我看到了你坚韧不拔的内心，因此要赐你新生的可能。

**Sunday:** Throughout these seventeen system hours, you will be unable to escape the Dreamscape or contact any of your companions. You only have two paths before you, and it all depends on whether you can complete my test within the time limit.

> **星期日：**这十七个系统时里，你无法离开梦境，也无法与任何同伴往来。你只有两条路可走，这取决于约定的时限内，你能否完成我的考验。

**Sunday:** Should you succeed, you will be able to coalesce into the Harmony and be with your Family. If you fail, you will suffer the wrath of the Eternal Centurion and fall into an abyss of doom.

> **星期日：**若成功，你便能融入谐乐，与万千家人同在；若失败，则将承受「无限夫长」的怒火，坠入万劫不复的深渊。所以我才特意强调了*暂时*二字。

**Aventurine:** Sounds like I'm going to end up the same either way.

> **砂金：**…该死的，听起来我的下场横竖都一样啊。

**Sunday:** I indeed do need a servant to help me uncover the evil hidden in The Family from an external perspective. I will purge the evil from the inside and bring the real culprits to justice within seventeen system hours.

> **星期日：**我确实需要一位仆人，助我从外部找出家族中潜伏的*邪恶*。而我会自内向外肃清，在十七个系统时内将真凶捉拿归案。

**Sunday:** When the time comes, compare your findings with mine. If both our findings align, or if you can provide me more insights... then THEY will truly be able to grant you mercy and honesty.

> **星期日：**等时候到了，就将你的发现同我核验。如果我们二人的判断一致，或者你能带给我更多…那祂便能将慈爱和诚实真正地施给你了。

**Aventurine:** Shameless hypocrites... You took everything from me and still demand the truth? That isn't fair. Your carnival reeks with the stench of cash. Nothing is achievable without it!

> **砂金：**…无耻的伪君子，你没收了我所有的东西，还要我给你真相？这不公平，在你们这座充满铜臭味的游乐园里，没钱办不成任何事。

**Sunday:** This is meant to be an act of personal virtue, not requiring The Family's support. Your bag is over there, do as you please. I believe you can trade this bag of worthless jewels for everything you need. That's what gamblers excel at, isn't it?

> **星期日：**这应当是你个人的义举，无需家族的援助。你的行李袋在那里，请便吧，相信你能用这袋低贱的珠宝换来一切。这是赌徒最擅长的事，不是么？

**Sunday:** Off you go, Mr. Aventurine. You are free. I will wait here for your good news.

**Sunday:** Why would I do that, Mr. Aventurine? I'm just wondering what a passerby who stumbled upon a scene of murder could have found out, that's all.

> **星期日：**怎么会，砂金先生，我真的只是想知道，一位*偶然*出现在她命案现场的*过客*能有什么*发现*，仅此而已。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Sunday:** By the way, before you go, I have a personal question.

> **砂金：**又怎么了？

> **星期日：**你……

> **卡卡瓦夏：**……

> **卡卡瓦夏：**…以后不会再有了。

> **？？？：**它是很重要，但更重要的是你，我最爱的弟弟…我不会责怪你，但你要记得妈妈说过的话……

> **？？？：**痛苦和贫穷是母神的考验，祂也赐给了我们机遇，那就是你的幸运，卡卡瓦夏。你的好运是我们…也是所有埃维金人最宝贵的财富。

## 063　When the Sacred Ginmill Closes　酒店关门之后

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：The Reverie (Reality)　|　角色：Trailblazer; Topaz and Numby; Himeko; March 7th; Gallagher; Welt; Acheron; Black Swan; Boothill; Alley　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Double Indemnity'' completed

### Inquire at the reception

> *You two make it to hotel in reality to gather more information about the victims of the two homicides. Hopefully, the receptionist won't stick to their professional conduct and will drop all the right clues.*

[Recap] You, Himeko, and March 7th inquired about the situation at the hotel reception and realized that news of Robin's death had been suppressed. The system also had no information about Firefly. At the same time, a group of IPC Workers arrived.

**Alley:** Umm, excuse me...

> **艾丽：**不好意思……

**Alley:** I can't seem to find any information on this artist in the Iris Family archives. The photo you provided also doesn't show any matches...

**Himeko:** ...Just as I thought. I'd like to ask, what kind of traces do people leave when they enter a dream?

> **姬子：**…这也在意料中。我想再请教下，人们入梦时通常会留下什么痕迹么？

**Alley:** Are you referring to the records when you enter the Dreampool? The equipment will monitor physiological indicators such as heart rate, blood oxygen levels, and body temperature in real time. This data will be included in statistics and handed over to The Family for the screening of any data anomalies. Immediate action will be taken once any illegal behavior is detected.

**Alley:** I apologize... The hotel does not have access. This information is managed by the Bloodhound Family. We can only gain access if there's a problem.

**March 7th:** Looks like nothing can be found here.

> **？？？：**救、救命…我的年终奖金都在那颗雪球上交代完了…我可不想自己的名字上部门的重大违纪通告啊！

**March 7th:** Hey, (Trailblazer), check it out! That voice... Could it be the IPC Workers from Belobog?

> **三月七：**喂，开拓者，你看！这声音…是贝洛伯格那帮公司员工？

**Team Leader:** To all guests: The IPC will be conducting special operations within the hotel. Please follow the staff in charge of evacuation to the designated safe zones, or compulsory measures will be enforced!

> **小组长：**敬告各位住客：星际和平公司将在酒店开展特殊工作，请跟随负责疏散的员工前往指定安全区域，否则将被采取强制措施！

**???:** I'll request a beating for you lot! You've been told not to drink during work hours!

> **？？？：**——请你个大头鬼！这群记吃不记打的家伙，都说了工作时间不要乱喝东西！

**Topaz:** Take him back to the hotel room!

> **托帕：**你们几个，赶紧把他拖下去，送回客房——

**Topaz:** ...I'll organize a meeting later to properly go over how this incident report should be written!

> **托帕：**——晚点我拉个会议，好好复盘下事故报告该怎么写！

**March 7th:** ...Miss Topaz! I never thought I'd run into you on Penacony.

> **三月七：**…托帕小姐！没想到会在匹诺康尼遇见你。

**Topaz:** Long time no see, Astral Express crew. Aventurine has told me a lot about your happenings... Hmm?

> **托帕：**好久不见，星穹列车的各位，你们的事我从砂金那里都听说啦…嗯？

**Topaz:** ...It's fine. Do as they ask, and try to avoid any conflicts with The Family... Report to me before taking any action. Mhm. Yes, alright.

> **托帕：**…没关系，就按他们要求的做，尽量避免和家族起冲突…采取任何行动前先和我汇报。嗯。嗯，好。

**Topaz:** ...As you see — the IPC isn't very popular here on Penacony. Cordiality from The Family is a mere facade. The former frontier prison has turned around and cuffed its shackles on the IPC staff now.

> **托帕：**唉…如你们所见，公司在匹诺康尼可是不太受欢迎。家族的地主之谊也只是表面客气，曾经的边陲监狱，如今要反过来给公司职员戴上镣铐啦。

**Topaz:** Only Aventurine, who carries an invitation is allowed to attend the banquet. An entourage like us... We can only sit around in the reality Hotel, unauthorized to even enter dreams.

> **托帕：**也只有带着「邀请函」的砂金被允许参加盛会，我们这些随行人员…连入梦资格都没有，只能在现实酒店停留。

**Himeko:** No wonder Aventurine's scrambling to partner up with someone — the IPC can't back him up in the Dreamscape.

> **姬子：**难怪砂金到处找人合作，原来他在梦境里得不到公司的援助。

**Topaz:** His situation isn't optimistic, I hear. You're all helping to investigate some... dirt on The Family, are you not? Let me know if you need anything outside the Dreamscape. The IPC always treats its partners well.

> **托帕：**听说他处境不太乐观，你们在帮忙调查一些…对家族不利的事，对吧？在梦境外边，有什么需要随时和我说，公司向来不会亏待合作伙伴。

**Himeko:** Thank you, Miss Topaz. We're on our way to the Hounds to verify some intel. Perhaps you've had dealings with them?

> **姬子：**谢谢你，托帕小姐。我们正要找猎犬们打听案情，也许你已经和他们打过交道了？

**Topaz:** Yep, they're tailing us right now. Why not go and talk to them... it'll take the spotlight off me, being constantly stared at is really uncomfortable.

> **托帕：**喏，就在后边跟着呢，找他们就行——正好，帮我转移下视线，一直被人盯着太难受啦。

### (Optional) Talk to Topaz

> *Although the investigation is heading nowhere, you happen to bump into Topaz and the loyal Bloodhound members who follow her everywhere to keep a close eye on the IPC people. You have read enough detective novels to know that police officers usually have no value except supporting the detective and providing key clues. It's time for them to play their part!*

[Recap] You spoke with Topaz. She said that Aventurine loved to take risks when doing business, and that his motto was "all or nothing." She did not know much about the case and requested for you to continue investigating.

*(Talk to Topaz, optional)*

**Topaz:** But Aventurine's luck has always been good. He's always closed all his cases without a hitch, and basically never lost a gamble. Which is why on the issue of retaking Penacony I'm watching with keen interest.

> **托帕：**不过砂金的运气确实很好，经手的案子大多圆满落幕，基本没赌输过。所以在收复匹诺康尼这件事上…我也拭目以待。

- ▸ It works only when all parties' interests are aligned.

**Topaz:** Of course. It's business, after all. What's important is where you're seated at the table.

> **托帕：**当然。交易嘛，重要的是你坐在桌子哪边。

**Topaz:** As for the two cases... Apologies, but I don't have much info on them either. All I can do is ask you to keep digging for more details.

> **托帕：**至于两起案件…抱歉，我手上的信息也不多。详情只能拜托各位继续调查了。

*(Talk to Topaz again, optional)*

**Topaz:** Please continue to investigate the case. All I can do is sit tight in the real world. I look forward to hearing your progress.

> **托帕：**拜托各位继续调查案情了，我在现实中等待你们的好消息。

> **三月七：**不不不不不用…你站在这儿就好了，听话哈……

**(Trailblazer):** (Looks like he still doesn't want to budge an inch...)

> **开拓者：**（看来他还是不打算松口……）

The principled Bloodhound Family member is mired in endless sorrow. As compensation, you have to think of a way to comfort him.

- ▸ Try reading his inner thoughts again.

[Recap] Under the effects of Clockwork, the guard's feelings turned to "joy." He noticed that it was time to clock out and left gleefully.

*(Tune Principled Bloodhound Family Member's emotion to Happy)*

**Principled Bloodhound Family Member:** Let me see what time it is now...

> **有原则的猎犬家系成员：**…什么，已经到点了？

**Principled Bloodhound Family Member:** ...Woah, it's this time already?

> **有原则的猎犬家系成员：**下班咯！你们谁也别想拦着我——！

**Principled Bloodhound Family Member:** Time to clock out! And no one's gonna stop meeee!

> **有原则的猎犬家系成员：**感谢您配合「家族」的工作。

The highly principled member of the Bloodhound Family laughed heartily and left the scene.

**March 7th:** ...

> **三月七：**……

**Himeko:** ...

> **姬子：**……

**March 7th:** (Trailblazer), this clockwork trick of yours... It's kinda dangerous.

> **三月七：**开拓者，你这钟表把戏怕是有点…危险了。

**Himeko:** ...At least he won't be getting in our way again. Let's go find that Gallagher and ask him the intricacies of the case.

> **姬子：**…至少他不会再拦着我们了。走吧，去找那位加拉赫，和他仔细聊聊案情。

### Talk to Gallagher

> *Seems like there are no more Bloodhounds obstructing your way, or they are simply too focused on the investigation to notice you two. Anyway, make use of this opportunity to talk to the Security Officer.*

[Recap] You met Gallagher. He said that the Astral Express was an esteemed guest of the Watchmaker and offered his condolences for what happened to Firefly. He beckoned you to follow him and talk about the case at another place.

**Gallagher:** I was wondering what all the commotion was... Oh, it's you guys. Welcome. Since you made it here, what can I do for you?

> **加拉赫：**我说怎么这么吵…是你们啊，欢迎。都找到这来了，有什么事？

**Himeko:** Hello, Mr. Gallagher, sir. Judging from your tone, it sounds like you were expecting us.

> **姬子：**加拉赫先生，您好。听您的语气，您知道我们会来这里？

**Gallagher:** Hahaha... Miss Himeko, you're too polite. There's no need to call me "Sir."

> **加拉赫：**哈哈哈…姬子小姐太客气了，对我不必用「您」。

**Himeko:** ...Mr. Gallagher, you even know my name?

> **姬子：**…加拉赫先生还知道我的名字？

**Gallagher:** Of course I do! You folks are from the legendary Astral Express, and honorable guests of the Watchmaker.

> **加拉赫：**知道，怎么不知道？你们可是大名鼎鼎的星穹列车，「钟表匠」的贵客。

**Gallagher:** I had an encounter with this gentleman/lady in the Golden Hour. I remember that little silver-haired girl was there too...

> **加拉赫：**我在黄金的时刻与这位先生/小姐有过一面之缘，记得当时那位银色头发的小姑娘也在……

**Gallagher:** ...I'm sorry for what happened to that kid.

> **加拉赫：**…我对那孩子的遭遇深表惋惜。

- ▸ That's exactly what The Family has ordered us to investigate.
- ▸ We are sorry too...

**Himeko:** This is also the reason why we've come to visit you, Mr. Gallagher. The Express can't just overlook the death of that child, so we've decided to help The Family get to the bottom of it, in the hopes of getting justice for her.

> **姬子：**这也是我们前来拜访加拉赫先生的理由。列车不能对那孩子的死坐视不理，决定协助家族查清真相，希望能为她讨个公道。

**Gallagher:** The Nameless involved with The Family... What an unpredictable twist of fate.

> **加拉赫：**无名客竟也和家族搅和在一起了…天意弄人啊。

**March 7th:** Why...? What's wrong with The Family?

> **三月七：**家族…怎么了吗？

**Gallagher:** It's nothing. On Penacony, everyone loves The Family.

> **加拉赫：**没什么，别在意。在匹诺康尼，所有人都喜欢家族。

**Gallagher:** No matter how much one resists the "beautiful dream," when the time comes, they too will find it hard to let go... Who wants to leave a warm nest? Just idiots, little kids... and inebriated fools.

> **加拉赫：**再怎么抗拒「美梦」的人，到了时候，也会变得舍不得。有谁会愿意离开温暖的窝？只有傻瓜、小孩子…还有脑袋不清醒的酒鬼。

**Himeko:** Mr. Gallagher seems to be getting at something.

> **姬子：**…加拉赫先生似乎意有所指。

**Gallagher:** You got it wrong, I'm not.

> **加拉赫：**你误会了，我没有。

**Gallagher:** You want to discuss the case? Sure, come with me. This is not a good place to talk. Let's go elsewhere.

> **加拉赫：**你们想聊案子？可以，跟我来吧，这地不适合说话，咱们挪个窝。

### Speak with Acheron

> *Once again, the focus shifts. Sometimes, you feel as though you're living in a movie — nothing else can explain the constant switch of the point-of-view.*

At this moment, on the other side...

[Recap] Welt and Acheron discussed Penacony itself. She believed that sweet dream was on the brink of collapse, a result of human nature. When one is immersed in a dreamscape of pure indulgence, that was their inevitable descent into death.

**Welt:** Even after that chilling tragedy, this dream is still running effortlessly.

> **瓦尔特：**即便发生了那样耸人听闻的惨案，这片美梦也依旧在有条不紊地运作啊。

**Welt:** Other than The Family of the Harmony, it's hard to imagine any other power in the universe that could sustain a building of such magnitude.

> **瓦尔特：**除了「同谐」的家族，很难想象宇宙中还有哪一方势力，能维系一座如此庞大的建筑。

**Acheron:** The Family itself is a huge, perfect building. Like... A living idol.

> **黄泉：**家族本身也是一座巨大、完美的建筑，就像…一尊活着的神像。

**Acheron:** Each member of The Family sees themselves as a piece of the divine puzzle, revolving around a singular core and a shared ideal. Under THEIR command, they loyally fulfill their roles, offering themselves while also receiving sustenance in return.

> **黄泉：**每位家族成员都将自己视作神体的一块拼图，围绕着唯一的核心、共同的理想，在祂的指挥下，忠诚地各司其职，奉献自我，同时又反受其给养。

**Welt:** Interesting analogy. Perhaps that's why Penacony's "beautiful dream" has persisted for so long.

> **瓦尔特：**很有趣的比喻，或许这就是匹诺康尼的「美梦」得以长存的根本。

**Acheron:** But the human body has its limits, and so does the divine's.

> **黄泉：**但人体终有其时，神躯亦然。

**Welt:** That doesn't sound like the kind of comment a Galaxy Ranger would make.

> **瓦尔特：**这就不像是一位「巡海游侠」会发表的评论了。

**Acheron:** Just pointing out the facts. Mr. Yang will definitely have a better sense of what's going on than I do.

> **黄泉：**只是点出事实，瓦尔特先生一定比我更能参透个中滋味。

**Welt:** Why do you say that, Miss Acheron?

> **瓦尔特：**黄泉小姐何出此言？

**Acheron:** The beautiful dream is crumbling, but not because of a particular Aeon, a particular faction, or a particular visitor. Its collapse stems from a certain inevitability of human nature. The Family refuses to acknowledge this, and it has ultimately backfired and become a catalyst...

> **黄泉：**美梦正在崩溃，但并不因为某柱星神、某个派系，或某位具体的来客。它的崩溃源自某种人性的必然，家族不愿承认这点，却在无形中反成了催化剂……

**Acheron:** As people immerse their souls in the Dreamscape, where consequences and pain cease to exist, and only ease and pleasure prevail, they draw closer and closer to necrosis. Regardless of the perceived bliss, death looms as the inevitable conclusion.

> **黄泉：**当人放任精神沉溺于无需代价，没有痛苦，只有安逸和享乐的梦境时，他们和「坏死」的距离便会越来越近。无论他认为自己活在何种极乐中，死亡都是无从改变的结局。

**Acheron:** Also, this necrosis will diffuse and spread. One piece of the puzzle's mutation will eventually cause the entire building to shake, break... and crumble.

> **黄泉：**并且，这种坏死会传播、扩散，一块拼图的异变最终会导致整座建筑的摇晃、破碎…崩坏。

**Welt:** ...In the end, the dreams that people built in the name of freedom became the cage that imprisoned them.

> **瓦尔特：**最后人们为自由而建的美梦，会反过来成为囚禁自我的牢笼。

**Welt:** I'm sure you've gained a lot from this trip, Miss Acheron. Are you willing to share your findings with me?

> **瓦尔特：**想必黄泉小姐此行收获不小，愿意同我分享一下吗？

**Acheron:** Of course... That's if I remember.

> **黄泉：**当然…前提是我还记得。

She says this as her hand gently rests on the hilt of the sword.

— And then quickly lets go, in the blink of an eye.

**Welt:** ...?

> **瓦尔特：**…？

**Acheron:** Don't mind me. It's just a habit. Owing to events in the past, I've become easily... forgetful. It's only when this sword is unsheathed that those hazy memories start to become clearer.

> **黄泉：**别在意，只是习惯。因为一些过往，我变得很容易…遗忘，只有当这柄刀出鞘时，那些朦胧的景象才会逐渐清晰。

**Welt:** Take your time.

> **瓦尔特：**请随意。

**Acheron:** That should do it. I vividly remember everything that occurred on Penacony. Ask away.

> **黄泉：**足够了，在匹诺康尼发生的事我记得很清楚。请问吧。

- ▸ Regarding the Moment of Daybreak...

**Welt:** The Moment of Daybreak — I've heard that's where the "Dawn Factory," which processes the foundation of the Dreamscape, is located.

> **瓦尔特：**黎明的时刻——我听说那里坐落着加工梦境基底的「早霞工厂」。

**Acheron:** Behind the Dreamscape's song and dance, stand many "imagination" factories. Workers create all kinds of whimsical works day in and day out in their dreams, then they return to reality and sleep on a narrow bed in a room — a far cry from luxury.

> **黄泉：**梦境的声色犬马背后，是一座座「想象」的工厂。工人们日复一日地在梦中创造各种奇思妙想的商品，然后回到现实中在与豪华客房相去甚远的卧榻上休息。

**Acheron:** They say it will suffice. Experiencing the bizarre and motley Dreamscape is the best reward.

> **黄泉：**他们说这就足够，光怪陆离的梦境已是最好的报酬。

**Acheron:** There, I encountered a young woman who had just come of age, the perfect time to indulge in beautiful dreams. Her greatest wish was to one day move to the Golden Hour and see the magnificent garments that she had woven with her own hands.

> **黄泉：**我在那里遇见一位少女，她刚成年，正是应该纵情享受美梦的年纪。她最大的愿望是有朝一日能迁至黄金的时刻，看看由自己的双手织就的华服。

**Acheron:** For certain reasons, her wish was difficult to fulfill. But I managed to bring her a garment.

> **黄泉：**由于某些原因，她的愿望很难实现。不过我想办法带了一件衣裳给她。

- ▸ Regarding the Gilded Hour...

**Welt:** Gilded Hour... It's said to be Penacony's Currency Center.

> **瓦尔特：**烫金的时刻…据说那里是匹诺康尼的货币中心。

**Acheron:** Yes... It is a fortress-like financial city, the economic heart of the Dreamscape. The Pepeshi people of the Alfalfa Family are there to keep it running, sending blood that is made from money everywhere on Penacony.

> **黄泉：**嗯…那是一座森严得如同要塞的金融之城，梦境的经济心脏。苜蓿草家系的皮皮西们在那里维持它的运转，将纸钞构成的血液送向巨人匹诺康尼的各处。

**Acheron:** Everyone there is exquisitely dressed and always in a hurry. The greatest wish of the local Pepeshi people is for their future generations to work in the Gilded Hour.

> **黄泉：**那里所有人都衣着精致，脚步匆匆。进入「烫金的时刻」工作，是当地皮皮西人对后代最大的愿望。

**Acheron:** I've never met anyone who was willing to talk. I could only stand at the crossroads, watching crowds of people hurrying like the wind through the jungle of steel, only to deposit the Alfalfa credits that they'd earned into the bank's vault.

> **黄泉：**我没有遇见任何愿意交谈的人。只能站在十字路口，看着一群又一群人像风一样匆匆掠过钢铁的丛林，只为把赚来的苜蓿币锁进银行金库。

**Acheron:** I don't know if they would open the vault door, but before I left, I witnessed a well-dressed Pepeshi person plummet from the sky while those around him continued on their way, unfazed.

> **黄泉：**我不知道他们是否还会打开那金库的门。但在我离开前，我亲眼目睹一位衣冠楚楚的皮皮西从空中跌落，而周围的人依旧穿过了他。

- ▸ About the Blue Hour...

**Welt:** I hear the Blue Hour is very romantic. Do you have any tales to share?

> **瓦尔特：**听说蓝调的时刻十分浪漫，黄泉小姐可曾见证了什么故事？

**Acheron:** Perhaps Mr. Yang has heard — there is a large boat called the "Eventide" anchored along the Sea of Dreams, where soft music and dancing persist endlessly every night.

> **黄泉：**也许瓦尔特先生也听说过，那里有一艘名为「黄昏号」的巨轮停泊在梦海之上，轻歌曼舞，夜夜不休。

**Acheron:** I ran into a wizened lady there. She was at the dock waiting for her long-departed lover to return, waiting for countless hours within time that stood still.

> **黄泉：**我在那里遇到一位苍老的妇人，她在港口盼着多年前离开的爱人归来，在停滞的时间里等待了无数的时间。

**Acheron:** In the humid sea breeze, she spoke of her own youth — like many who desired wealth and love, they came to Penacony to pursue their dreams. Alas, her lover's consciousness was lost in the dark depths of the Sea of Dreams.

> **黄泉：**潮湿的海风里，她谈起自己的青春——就像许多渴望财富与机遇的人们，他们为了追逐梦想来到匹诺康尼，他的意识却消失在了梦海深处。

**Acheron:** Finally, she suggested we continue our conversation on a boat in the shallows. I agreed, and boarded the boat with her, but she never said anything, her eyes absent-mindedly gazing at the horizon for what seemed like forever.

> **黄泉：**末了，她提议坐上舢板，在近海上继续我们的对谈。我答应了，同她一起登上小船，可她却再没说些什么，只是茫然地望了海天相接的尽头许久。

**Acheron:** Finally, we retreated to the beach.

> **黄泉：**最后，我们退回了沙滩。

- ▸ About the Moment of Dusk...

**Welt:** The Dreamscape of chic, luxury and consumerism — the Moment of Dusk — my companions have been there too.

> **瓦尔特：**时尚、奢侈与消费主义的梦境，薄暮的时刻，我的同伴也拜访了那里。

**Acheron:** Then you all must have seen those who are attempting to realize their dreams, or have realized them, scattering money as if it were dust, and betting it on all or nothing. Everything has a price, and everything can be bought or sold — even dreams themselves.

> **黄泉：**那你们一定见识过为实现梦想、或已然实现梦想的人们在那里挥金如土、孤注一掷。一切皆可标价，一切皆可买卖——哪怕是梦想本身。

**Acheron:** I saw an Intellitron there who was preparing to auction himself. When someone wins a bid, under stipulated periods and rules, he would do the buyer's every bidding, becoming that person's very possession.

> **黄泉：**我在那里看见一位智械，他准备的拍品是「自我」。一旦有人竞拍成功，在约定的期限和规则下，他会践行买家的一切指示，成为那人绝对的所有物。

**Acheron:** That Intellitron had been auctioned off a dozen times, and I participated in his thirteenth. That was the grandest banquet I had ever attended, but never again did anyone cast another glance at him.

> **黄泉：**那智械一共被拍卖了十二次，我参加了他的第十三次拍卖会。那是我见过最人声鼎沸的盛宴，但再也没人将目光投向他。

**Acheron:** This time around, there were no successful bids for him.

> **黄泉：**这一次，他流拍了。

- ▸ That's everything.

**Acheron:** This is what I've seen and heard along the way...

> **黄泉：**这便是我这一路浅浅的见闻……

[Recap] Welt told Acheron a story from his homeland — a man once wove together everyone's dreams and shouldered that burden himself to save the entire world. This story overlapped with Acheron's past.

**Acheron:** Someone once said to me: Penacony wasn't like this a long time ago, nor should it be.

> **黄泉：**曾有人这么对我说：匹诺康尼在很久以前并非如此，匹诺康尼也不应如此。

**Acheron:** I've traveled through the reality and Dreamscape of the Planet of Festivities, watched the tides of night rise and fall, when time stopped for people. Where the spirits of the rich and impoverished... remain forever fixed on their own scales.

> **黄泉：**我一路走过盛会之星的现实和梦境，看着黑夜升起又落下，时光为人们停驻，而精神的富有和贫穷…也永远停留在各自的刻度。

**Acheron:** This is why I think the collapse of the beautiful dream is inevitable.

> **黄泉：**所以我认为「美梦」的崩溃是必然。

**Welt:** There might be a way to change everything.

> **瓦尔特：**也许有办法改变这一切。

**Acheron:** Perhaps. But if this is indeed the world that people desire — if this is precisely why life chooses to slumber — should we still seek to change it?

> **黄泉：**也许吧。但如果这正是人们所期望的世界——如果这正是生命选择沉睡的原因——我们还应令它做出改变吗？

**Welt:** ...

> **瓦尔特：**……

**Welt:** ...Miss Acheron, now it's my turn to share a story with you.

> **瓦尔特：**…黄泉小姐，换我来为你分享一个故事吧。

**Welt:** There was a man from my homeland who, at a time when the world was grappling with deep, unhealable pain, made a choice.

> **瓦尔特：**在我的故乡有一个男人凯文，在世界面临难以愈合的伤痛之际，他做出了一个决定。此处BGM为For Kevin，收录于崩坏3OST《跨越终焉之日》

**Welt:** He wove together the dreams of everyone in the world, linking people's dreamscapes, and shouldered this burden himself. From this, he created a giant, a "Spiritual Adam."

> **瓦尔特：**他将世界上所有人的梦编织在一起，将人与人的梦境彼此连缀，再以己身背负，他由此创造出一名巨人，一位「精神的亚当」。详见《崩坏3》主线间章「圣痕计划」

**Welt:** Since that moment, the giant stood between heaven and earth, becoming the pillar of the world's existence. As a price, those who found it hard to move forward, who could not advance... forever lost their "future."

> **瓦尔特：**从此，那巨人立于天地之间，成为整个世界存续的支柱。而作为代价，那些难以前进，无法前进的人…他们将永远失去「未来」。

**Welt:** They slumbered in a dream devoid of disaster and pain, living out their days peacefully in the man's created utopia. And it is because of the wishes of those people, who wished not to awaken, that this Spiritual Adam became unbreakable.

> **瓦尔特：**他们沉眠于没有灾难和苦痛的梦里，在男人创造的理想乡中度过安然一生。而「精神的亚当」会因这些人不愿醒来的愿望…变得坚不可摧。

**Acheron:** And yet, you stand here right now, which also means... that man failed.

> **黄泉：**但如今你却站在此处，这也就代表…那个男人失败了。

**Welt:** Because people must always move towards the future. Even if human weaknesses make them pause, when they truly cannot move forward... humanity will eventually seek a way to save itself.

> **瓦尔特：**因为人们总要走向未来。纵使人性的弱点让他们驻足停步，但在真正无法前行的时候…人类一定会试图拯救自己。

**Welt:** And that man... He was NEVER a failure. Like everyone in that world, he etched the possibilities of human nature into his heart.

> **瓦尔特：**而那个男人…他也从来不是失败者。他与那世界的每个人一样，将人性的可能铭记于心。

**Welt:** He was the sunchaser of legend, soaring towards the sky, and embracing his final victory with his fall.

> **瓦尔特：**他是神话中的逐日者，向天飞翔，并以坠落迎来自己最终的胜利。

**Welt:** He ascended to heights uncharted, only to come face to face with the sun — a place not visited by anyone before. His wings would melt because of it, only for him to fall into the sea, and after that...

> **瓦尔特：**他高高升起，只为来到太阳面前，那是没有任何人曾经到达的地方。他将因之融化，陨落大海，而在那之后……

**Welt:** ...countless others would surpass him, soaring to even greater heights.

> **瓦尔特：**…将有无数的人越过他的身躯，飞上更高的天际。详见《崩坏3》主线35章

**Acheron:** ...A fitting metaphor for the Nameless' Trailblazing spirit.

> **黄泉：**…很符合无名客的「开拓」精神。

**Acheron:** Thank you, Mr. Yang. I know what you wish to confirm. The universe has innumerable similar, yet different worlds. In these worlds, there are innumerable people who look alike, yet don't.

> **黄泉：**谢谢，瓦尔特先生。我知道你想确认什么。宇宙中有着无数相似却又相异的世界。在这些世界中，也有无数相似却又相异的人。

**Acheron:** I, too have embarked on journeys, encountering "old friends" with familiar faces on different worlds, witnessing their destinies follow paths similar to mine. So, I will tell you...

> **黄泉：**我也曾踏上旅途，在不同的世界邂逅容貌相似的「故人」，目睹他们的命运行过似曾相识的轨迹。所以，我会告诉你……

**Acheron:** Even if not completely similar, this story you just told... It overlaps with my past. And within that abyssal dream...

> **黄泉：**尽管不完全相同，但你所描绘的这个故事…它和我的过往重叠在一起。而在那深不见底的梦中……

**Acheron:** ...I ended that man's life, alone.

**Welt:** ...

> **瓦尔特：**……

**Acheron:** I am not who you think I am, nor will my home be as fortunate as your world.

> **黄泉：**我不是你认识的那个人，我的故乡也未能像你们的世界那样幸运。

**Welt:** ...I am sorry.

> **瓦尔特：**…我很遗憾。

**Acheron:** It's fine. I don't mind, so long as I can alleviate your suspicions.

> **黄泉：**没关系，如果这能消解你的疑虑，我不介意。

**Welt:** ...There's something I still wish to know, Miss Acheron. Under that representation of The Hunt, exactly what sort of power is it that has motivated your solitary journey thus far?

> **瓦尔特：**但我仍想知道，在那「巡猎」的表象下，黄泉小姐，究竟是*哪一种力量*驱使着你独行至今。

**Acheron:** ...Mr. Yang, before answering that question, I wish to continue the previous topic.

> **黄泉：**…瓦尔特先生，在回答这个问题前，我想先继续刚才的话题。

**Acheron:** I like your analogy very much. Indeed, birds are born to fly, but in a distant past, their ancestors could only gaze at the sky in envy.

> **黄泉：**我很喜欢你的比喻。诚然，鸟儿生来就会飞翔，但在遥远的曾经，它们的祖先也只得从地面仰望高天。

**Acheron:** They saw that faraway ray of light from above the heavens, piercing through the clouds and blanketing the earth. And so, time and time again, generation after generation, the birds spread their wings and took to the sky, attempting to touch its ceiling — all because the sun was there.

> **黄泉：**它们看见那遥远的，来自天外的光芒洞穿云翳，普照大地。于是一次又一次，一代又一代，鸟儿们展翅高翔，试图触碰天顶，只因太阳就在那里。

**Acheron:** Then, what if the last bird finally soars into the sky, only to realize that the end of the light is not the sun, but darkness...

> **黄泉：**那么，如果当最后的鸟儿终于飞上天际，却看见光芒的尽头并非太阳，而是漆黑的大日……

**Acheron:** Then why exactly... do we even walk towards the light?

> **黄泉：**那我们究竟是为了什么…才要向光而行？

### Recall the memories on Acheron

> *Once again, the focus shifts. Sometimes, you feel as though you're living in a movie — nothing else can explain the constant switch of the point-of-view.*

12

**Black Swan:** ...

> **黑天鹅：**……

**???:** Long time no see! Having fun on Penacony, Acheron?

**Black Swan:** (Imposter? I see. She gave my whereabouts to someone who is tracking Acheron too.)
