**Black Swan:** Who are you?

> **黑天鹅：**你是谁？

**???:** Huh? Did I make a mistake? Who the fudge are you?

> **？？？：**嗯？我打错了？宝了个贝的，你又是谁？

**Black Swan:** I'm the Garden of Recollection's Memokeeper.

> **黑天鹅：**我是流光忆庭的忆者。

**???:** Heh! Not bad. This is the kind of tough challenge I like. Are you that imposter's bodyguard? Never mind. It's fine. I'll leave a round for you, so get that forehead clean and wait for me!

> **？？？：**嚯！不错，我就喜欢这种硬茬。你是那个冒牌货的保镖？还是别的什么人？算了，无所谓。我也会留一发子弹给你的，洗干净脑门等着吧。

**Black Swan:** I don't know what you're talking about — but you know Acheron, the Galaxy Ranger, yes? I have something to ask you.

> **黑天鹅：**我不知道你在说什么——但你认识黄泉，那个巡海游侠，对么？我有事要问你。

**???:** Haha, are you asking me to write your will? Sure, go ahead.

> **？？？：**哈哈，要我帮你写遗嘱？可以，你说吧。

**Black Swan:** Not quite. I only want to ask, how exactly did she become a Galaxy Ranger?

> **黑天鹅：**不是什么遗嘱——我只想问你，她究竟是如何*变成*巡海游侠的。

**???:** ...

> **？？？：**……

**Black Swan:** She's clearly not a Pathstrider of The Hunt. But you are, aren't you? Tell me, what's Acheron's deal?

> **黑天鹅：**她根本不是「巡猎」的命途行者，你才是，对么？告诉我，黄泉究竟是什么来头。

**???:** Hahahaha, sure! Never thought I'd come across an ally. Fudge, what a stroke of luck!

> **？？？：**…哈哈哈哈哈，可以！没想到是友军，他宝贝了个腿的，看来我真是撞大运了。

**???:** I'll be on Penacony soon. Memokeeper, go buy a bottle of Asdana's White Oak and warm it up, and I'll raise a glass to you.

> **？？？：**我马上就到匹诺康尼了，忆者，去买瓶「阿斯德纳白橡木」，温好，敬你一杯。

**???:** That lady's past? Nobody knows. But if all you want is a simple answer, sure, you best get a chair and take a seat. That woman named Acheron...

> **？？？：**那女人的过去？没人知道。但如果你要的只是个简单的答案，可以，你最好找张椅子垫在下面，那个叫黄泉的女人——

**???:**—is an Emanator who should not exist.

> **？？？：**——是个*不该存在*的令使。

Switching to Aventurine's POV...

Aventurine seems to have lost everything, but he still decides to hand out jewels and money on the street to make the most of what he has left. What is he trying to achieve...?

## 064　Heaven is a Place on Earth　人间天堂

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：Golden Hour　|　角色：Aventurine; Dr. Ratio; Chabro; Larose; Bocchi; Woolsey; Uma; Barrle (Golden Hour); Sparkle　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''When the Sacred Ginmill Closes'' completed

### Talk to Dr. Ratio

> *Through a sneaky magic trick, you get your hands on the gift money and your cornerstone, the Aventurine. Things get ugly, but your plan is still being executed at a timely pace. Not even the impending death can stop you. Distribute the gift money to the pedestrians to exchange for the intel you need.*

[Recap] Aventurine talked to Dr. Ratio and obtained a note from him. He said that all the Cornerstones had been placed where they should be, and he even recovered his prize. He was only a step away from victory.

**Aventurine:** ...

**Dr. Ratio:** You look pale. Or, is that also part of your act?

> **真理医生：**脸色很差啊。还是说，这也是你的演技？

**Aventurine:** Didn't think you'd have the nerve to show yourself.

> **砂金：**我没想到你还有脸来见我。

**Dr. Ratio:** I thought this was exactly what you wanted. After all, I faithfully fulfilled my duties as you instructed.

> **真理医生：**我以为这才是你想要的结果，毕竟我可是像你说的那样——「忠实地履行了自己的职责」。

**Dr. Ratio:** Just tell me if you can't hold on any longer.

> **真理医生：**你要是挺不住了，记得先通知我一声。

**Aventurine:** So, the "genius" of the Council of Mundanites wants to be my undertaker now? My... what an honor.

> **砂金：**庸众院的「天才」是打算替我收尸？天啊…真是荣幸。

**Dr. Ratio:** Yeah, and I'm pretty sure the people at the Strategic Investment Department would love to be notified of your death in due time, but let's not forget you won't be seeing them, because I'm the manager of this task.

> **真理医生：**战略投资部的各位一定也非常希望能及时收到你的死讯。别忘了，你再也没法见到他们了，这个任务现在可是落在我的头上。

**Aventurine:** Great, then tell your people that Aventurine is ready to go in seventeen system hours.

> **砂金：**好啊，那麻烦你现在就去报个信吧。就说「砂金已经做好万全准备，十七个系统时后即可入场。」

**Dr. Ratio:** You've got a lot of nerve — how exactly do you plan on completing your task while your hands are tied by the Harmony?

> **真理医生：**大言不惭。你打算怎么在被「同谐」禁锢的情况下完成任务？

**Aventurine:** My conversation with Sunday convinced me that there's a traitor in The Family, and that they hold the secrets of Penacony... So, I took the opportunity to set everything in motion.

> **砂金：**和星期日的对谈让我确信了家族中有叛徒，而匹诺康尼的秘密就在那人手中…借此机会，我也把所有基石放在了它们该在的位置上。

**Aventurine:** I even managed to recover the gift money. Things haven't gone this smoothly since I walked through the doors of The Reverie... Now, I'm only one step away from victory. Let's just wait and see.

> **砂金：**而现在我还成功拿回了礼金，自打踏入白日梦酒店的大门，事情就没像这样顺利过…看着吧，距离胜利我只差一步之遥了。

**Dr. Ratio:** Sounds like a very elaborate way of saying that you failed.

> **真理医生：**听起来你只是把自己的惨状复述了一遍，用的还是极其嘴硬的方式。

**Aventurine:** That's all I can say. Have you forgotten, Doctor? You betrayed me.

> **砂金：**我能说的就这些。忘了吗？你已经背叛过我了，教授。

**Aventurine:** Go, do what you must. I look forward to the sight of the IPC fleet surrounding Penacony. You've achieved what you desired, haven't you?

> **砂金：**去你该去的地方吧，我迫不及待想看到公司舰队包围匹诺康尼的样子了。你也拿到你想要的东西了，不是么？

**Dr. Ratio:** That's true, but what's your plan? Did you conceal an Orbital Support beacon in that gift money bag?

> **真理医生：**确实，但怎么着？你那袋礼金里，还藏了呼叫近地轨道支援的信标不成？

**Aventurine:** Well, who knows? Maybe that's why I'm handing out cash even when I'm about to bite the dust.

> **砂金：**搞不好呢？兴许这就是我死到临头还想着发钱的原因。

**Dr. Ratio:** ...You are indeed a gambler — an insane one at that.

> **真理医生：**…你彻底疯了，该死的赌徒。

**Aventurine:** Well, maybe I am. Who knows?

> **砂金：**也许我早疯了，谁知道呢？

**Dr. Ratio:** ...

> **真理医生：**……

**Dr. Ratio:** Fine. Here, take this. Open it when you're on your last legs. You'll thank me.

> **真理医生：**算了。给你这个，拿着。死到临头再打开它，你会感谢我的。

**Aventurine:** What's this...? Medical advice?

> **砂金：**这什么玩意…医嘱？

**Aventurine:** You catch on pretty fast, Doctor.

> **砂金：**…呵，你是懂戏剧性的，教授。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** Asking me to solve a case without giving a single clue... How typical of you, you wing-headed scoundrel.

> **砂金：**要我探案，又不给半点线索…真有你的，脑袋长翅膀的混蛋。

**Aventurine:** But the way you're all on edge about that stowaway, it's just as I guessed it would be. As for now... let the rain of wealth from the IPC fall evenly on everyone.

> **砂金：**但你们为那个偷渡犯如坐针毡的样子，倒是应了我的*猜想*。接下来…就让公司的财富之雨平等地落在每个人头上吧。

[Recap] Aventurine gave the gem to a passer-by and asked him about "Death." Chabro alleged that he had experienced it in a dream bubble, it felt like a monster covered with eyes had stabbed him in the stomach, and everything thereafter was just intense vertigo.

*(Talk to Chabro)*

**Chabro:** The world has truly lost its way...

- ▸ Here, I got these gems for you.

**Chabro:** You youngsters are always looking for a quick way to get an audience, but you know what? A truly great show never comes easy.

**Chabro:** Ah, I see! Another fearless youngster looking for death, huh? Well, let me give you a piece of advice. Don't think you're the first one who's ever thought of that idea.

> **夏布罗：**哦！又是个「不怕死」的年轻人。给你句忠告，别觉得自己的想法有多么超前。

> **夏布罗：**死亡？这类题材早烂大街了，我还在爱德华医生那儿买到过呢。说是什么…只此一份的高档匿名捐赠品，实际烂极了，都是噱头。

**Chabro:** The effects were awful. First, some monster covered in eyes stabs you in the gut, and then all you see are blurry glimpses of buildings and lights. The sky was spinning so fast it almost made me puke...

> **夏布罗：**效果太差劲，一开始是个长满眼睛的怪物照你肚子来了一刀，之后除了能模模糊糊地看到点高楼和灯光，就只剩下天旋地转了，差点给我晕吐出来……

> **夏布罗：**不然呢？别太指望匹诺康尼电影业的基本盘…他们还管这种东西叫作「先锋艺术」呢，可笑吧？

**Aventurine:** ...What a joke. Well, I'll leave you be, then. I hope you have a wonderful day!

> **砂金：**…说的是啊。不打扰您了，祝您度过愉快的一天。

> **砂金：**（长满眼睛的怪物，这描述倒是和那忆域迷因很像，可高楼和灯光…这些东西和「死亡」没什么关系吧。估计只是个用流言蜚语拼凑出来的梦泡吧。）

**Aventurine:** ...What do you mean?

> **花火：**你比我更清楚呀，是谁眼巴巴地看着唱不出歌儿的小鸟横死在面前？当然是你啦，小孔雀～

> **花火：**明知故问，因为你也快要和她一样，永远说不出话了呗。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

> **花火：**不过嘛，这在我看来倒不失为好事一桩，因为……

> **花火：**你只要知道我有这个本事就行。

**Aventurine:** I'm afraid I'll have to decline your offer. Who knows if your little gadget will actually work? By the way, I have no plans to search for the other mute friend you speak of, but it's good to hear that he's still here in Penacony.

> **砂金：**恐怕我得拒绝你的提议了，谁知道你这小玩具到底有没有用？顺便，我也不打算去找你口中的「另一位哑巴朋友」，但我很乐于听到这人还在匹诺康尼。

> **砂金：**剩下的我自己会办成：我会给家族的垮台准备一场伟大的揭幕表演。等到了最高潮，高墙将崩塌、人们将惊醒，不能说话的人也将重新开口——

**Aventurine:** When that time comes, go ahead and press the button, and light up the sky with a magnificent fireworks display for me. Catch you later, Fool.

> **砂金：**——等到了那个时候，就请你按下按钮，放个大烟花为我助兴吧。回见，愚者。

**Sparkle:** You're still talking big... But sure, if that happens, I'll stay true to my word.

> **花火：**到了这份上还有心思大放厥词…不过，一言为定啦。

**Sparkle:** Just don't let me down now, okay?

> **花火：**你可千万，别让我失望哦？

...

**Aventurine:** ...

> **冷漠的男性：**回来了啊，35号，喜欢你的护身符么？

> **砂金：**…「商品编码」也能当做护身符么？

**Indifferent Male:** Silence. I didn't give you permission to speak, you Sigonian hound.

> **冷漠的男性：**但我认为你很幸运，就把你买下了。从今往后，你和你的运气都是我的资产，明白了么？

**Indifferent Male:** Your first task is simple. In addition to you, I've purchased thirty... well, thirty-four other slaves.

**Indifferent Male:** Go and play a "game" with them. If you come out alive after two days, it proves that you're the real deal.

> **冷漠的男性：**去跟他们玩场「游戏」——两天时间，活着出来，证明你的本事货真价实。

**Indifferent Male:** Heh, testing out if you're a good product.

> **砂金：**你就不怕这钱白花了？

**Indifferent Male:** I've got stacks on stacks, blondie. The slave market is never short of self-righteous brats like you.

**Indifferent Male:** But you look good, and that's why many customers are betting their fortunes on a scrawny brat like you. So, go along now, and don't let your master down.

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 065　Why Do the Heathen Rage?　外邦为何争闹？

> 世界：Penacony　|　区域：The Reverie (Dreamscape)　|　角色：Gallagher; Siobhan; Trailblazer; Himeko; March 7th; Amiky　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Heaven is a Place on Earth'' completed

### Head to the Dreamjolt Hostelry

> *Gallagher gestures for everyone to adjourn the discussion to somewhere else. He brings you to the Dreamjolt Hostelry — A quiet and ordinary corner in this frolicking dream.*

[Recap] Gallagher brought all of you to the Dreamjolt Hostelry, where the drinksmith Siobhan welcomed you. Gallagher asked you to bring back some of your favorite ingredients, and he'd prepare a special drink for his guests from afar.

**???:** What brings you here, Gallagher?

> **？？？：**加拉赫，什么风把你给吹来了？

**Gallagher:** Some friends from the old days. Do you have a moment to spare, Siobhan?

> **加拉赫：**几位老朋友找上门。现在有空吗，舒翁？

**Siobhan:** I have the whole day to spare. Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to Dreamjolt Hostelry!

> **舒翁：**我都空了一天啦。各位，欢迎光临「惊梦酒吧」——

**Siobhan:** This bar offers a wide variety of drinks, but we draw the line at SoulGlad. Why settle for ordinary when you can experience extraordinary? We're dedicated to serving up nothing but pure joy and laughter.

### Listen to Gallagher's story

> *Effortlessly, Gallagher presents you with an exquisite drink. Hopefully, the drink can keep your conversation with him from getting too boring.*

[Recap] Gallagher discussed the case, confirming that Firefly was indeed an infiltrator, but she had gone missing both in the dreamscape and in reality. This had never happened before. The Family considered the Watchmaker an enemy, because he was the most humiliating stain on the Penacony Branch's history, the beginning of all the anomalies in the dreamscape. This renowned Watchmaker was in fact Mikhail.

**Gallagher:** Alright, let's start with what we know based on the clues The Family has. It seems that Firefly isn't a local or an invited guest. In other words... she's a stowaway.

> **加拉赫：**先说结论吧：根据家族手上的线索，这位流萤确实不是本地人，也不是受邀前来的宾客。换言之…她是个货真价实的偷渡犯。

**Gallagher:** She managed to fool me at first — my age must be getting the best of me. But here, on the Planet of Festivities, stowaways are a common sight. You're bound to run into one sooner or later. After the incident, the Hounds wasted no time searching for that girl in both the Dreamscape and reality.

> **加拉赫：**我也被这姑娘骗了，当真是年纪大咯。不过在盛会之星，偷渡不是多么稀罕的事，也不难查。事发后猎犬们立即采取了行动，从梦境和现实两头开始追踪。

**Gallagher:** But here's the thing... We only received bad news, and the trickiest kind at that.

> **加拉赫：**可结果…只有一个坏消息，也是最让人头疼的消息。

**Gallagher:** She simply vanished, leaving no trace in the Dreamscape or reality, as if she had never come to Penacony at all.

> **加拉赫：**这小姑娘人间蒸发了，梦里没留下任何痕迹，现实中也完全找不到身体，仿佛从没来过匹诺康尼。

- ▸ Is she a ghost!?

**Gallagher:** Let me be frank... This case, actually... is unlike anything the Bloodhound Family has dealt with before.

### Head to the Clock Studios Theme Park

> *To better get his story across, Gallagher decides to make things fun by taking everyone to Clock Studios Theme Park. Using the facilities there as tools, he will give you all a lesson on the history of Penacony... Everyone understands that you are exhausted, but they hope you won't doze off.*

Some time later...

**Gallagher:** Here we are, Clock Studios Theme Park, the most popular entertainment center in Penacony.

> **加拉赫：**喏——「克劳克影视乐园」，匹诺康尼最受欢迎的影视娱乐中心。

> **三月七：**不是要讲「钟表匠」的事吗？我还以为会去资料室之类的地方，怎么是这里……

**Gallagher:** The culture of a city reflects its history in the most authentic way. To you, it's a fun place, but to me, it's a prison for the planet's past.

> **加拉赫：**一座城市的文化就是历史最真实的注脚。在你眼中这是玩乐的地方，但在我眼中，它是一座监牢，用来监禁这颗星球的过往。

**Gallagher:** You know that Penacony used to be the IPC's prison planet, right? All the prisoners were brought here, helping the Garden of Recollection salvage the leaking memoria from the Macro Void...

> **加拉赫：**你们肯定知道匹诺康尼曾经是公司的监狱星，对吧？犯人们被押送来这里，帮流光忆庭打捞大孔洞里泄漏的忆质……

**Gallagher:** The prolonged exposure to high concentrations of memoria caused a unique phenomenon. The dreams of countless prisoners intersected and overlapped, and people started meeting each other in their dreams, living lives that were almost identical to reality.

> **加拉赫：**监狱长期暴露在高浓度的忆质中，产生了一种特殊的现象。无数个体的梦境交错重叠，人们开始在梦中相会，过上恍如现实的生活。

**Gallagher:** But everything has a price, and sweet dreams are no exception. In the end, the dream world was unable to alleviate the suffering of prisoners in reality. One of the prisoners broke free from the IPC's shackles and fought for freedom...

> **加拉赫：**但凡事总有代价，美梦也不例外。最后，梦境也无法消解人们现实中的苦难，在一位囚犯的带领下，边陲监狱砸碎了公司的镣铐，开始为自由而战……

**Gallagher:** ...He is Hanu, the great leader of Dreamville, the great peacemaker, and the faithful companion of the underdogs.

### Look for clues

> *You two manage to sneak into The Family's council chamber almost unhindered. It looks like a place laden with confidential documents, so you two can surely find something here.*

*(Investigate bookshelf on the right)*

*(Quick Read)*

A Letter from Robin

Dear Brother, How are you doing these days? I had intended to visit you at Dewlight Pavilion as soon as possible upon my return, but with the approaching Charmony Festival and your busy schedule, I refrained from troubling you. However, an urgent matter compels me to share something with you immediately. Since my return to Penacony, I have experienced a peculiar change in my voice. At first, I thought it was caused by exhaustion or illness, but after consulting with doctors, they assured me of my perfect health and dismissed my concerns. However, my voice worsened over time, and I even experienced periods of complete voice loss. In order to find answers, I conducted many private investigations (using my idle time out of rehearsals, of course). Eventually, I realized that the Harmony in Penacony is not pure. A discord lurking within has tainted my voice of Harmony, which I believe to be the root cause of my vocal issues. I immediately realized that such levels of interference can only occur if either a powerful external force is pulling the strings, or if a senior member of The Family is involved. Unfortunately, further investigation has led me to the latter conclusion. This is an extremely alarming discovery: A traitor has emerged within The Family in Penacony, and it is highly likely that this person is one of the four family heads. (I trust you implicitly, dear brother, because of our promise). With the Charmony Festival on the horizon, I fear this person intends to impede its progress, or even use the festival for some ulterior motive. At any rate, I suggest you monitor the other family heads while also prioritizing your own safety. You are the only true family member I have left. There is another matter that requires our attention. During my investigation, I learned about the Memory Zone Meme "Death," and my further inquiries led me to believe that the culprit who directed it to cause this series of incidents is likely the aforementioned traitor in The Family. I have collected more clues and am prepared to verify my hypothesis. Rest assured, you can just focus on the preparations for the Charmony Festival. Once I've thoroughly investigated "Death," I'll come and meet you immediately. It won't take too long. Given your heavy workload, please take care of yourself. Don't stay in the Dreamscape all the time. Spend some time in reality when you are free. I've brought some more specialties from other galaxies: Giantmoa pudding tarts from Morillons, wild strawberries from Akonyako (known for their exceptional size and sweetness, which I'm certain you'll enjoy), and almond meringue cream cracknels from Medicia. Don't forget to enjoy them! May Xipe be with us! Your sister, Robin

**Welt:** As soon as I and the rest of the Crew arrived in Penacony, Mr. Sunday and Robin showed up to greet us. I remember hearing something unusual in her voice... and now it seems I was right.

> **瓦尔特：**列车组刚刚抵达匹诺康尼不久，星期日先生就带着知更鸟小姐前来接待了我们。那时我便觉得她的声音有一丝古怪…现在看来，确有其事。

**Acheron:** Robin believed it was because the Harmony had been tampered with somehow, but as far as I know, there aren't many entities capable of interfering with the power of Paths.

> **黄泉：**知更鸟小姐认为这是因为「同谐」受到了某种干扰。但据我所知，能够干涉命途伟力的存在少之又少。

**Welt:** Meaning...

A Letter from Alfalfa

To Sunday, I have been informed about Robin, and I would like to express my deepest condolences. However, I must remind you that you now hold the position of not only her elder brother but also the head of the Oak Family. Your every action has implications for all of Penacony. As Penacony is going through a critical period, it is crucial that you do not allow your hatred to cloud your judgment, and be cautious not to engage in activities that others can use against you as leverage. I heard that you are planning to dedicate a significant amount of time and resources to find "Death." Such action does not serve the best interests of The Family as a whole, and I strongly advise you to reconsider it to avoid potential impeachments from other family heads. While you are convinced that "Death" is connected to the Watchmaker, I have met that Watchmaker many times long before the Dreammaster adopted you and your sister, and I have never found any evidence linking him to that Memory Zone Meme. Now that you are the head of the Oak Family, it is essential that you assess the situation objectively and consider the bigger picture. It is unwise to allocate all of Penacony's resources and manpower for the sake of a personal vendetta, as this would bring dishonor to the Great One! The Charmony Festival is on the horizon, and the Watchmaker's guests are all barely holding back their own agendas. Neither you nor I could face the severe consequences if Dominicus' arrival is delayed. Therefore, I urge you to control your emotions and fulfill your responsibilities as the head of the Oak Family, focusing only on the Festival and avoiding any interference from external sources. In addition, we must not neglect the honored guests invited by the Watchmaker, as mishandling this matter could lead to diplomatic conflicts with other major factions, involving us in disputes that could have been avoided. As your elder, I hope you comprehend the gravity of the situation and handle it appropriately. As for the matter of Robin, there would be time to pursue it once the Festival concludes. By then, I will provide you with the necessary resources and manpower in the name of the Alfalfa Family to help you get your revenge. Additionally, I have heard rumors that the Dreammaster is not entirely pleased with your recent activities. I advise you to conduct yourself with caution. Yours sincerely, Old Oti

[Recap] Aventurine met a child wandering alone, a young kid who claimed that he was looking for his parents in the theme park. He noticed the child's eye color, which was unique to the Avgins.

*(Approach Young Child)*

**Aventurine:** I thought minors weren't allowed in Golden Hour... Hey kid, are you okay? Are you lost—

> **年幼的孩子：**它们很漂亮，对吧？姐姐说，那是「芬戈妈妈」的礼物。彩色的眸子能给人带来好运。

> **年幼的孩子：**啊，先生…你也有双漂亮的眼睛。真好看！

**Aventurine:** Are... you alone? Where are your parents?

> **砂金：**你…就一个人吗？你的父母呢？

**Young Child:** They're in that amusement park. Papa and Mama went in first. I'm just about to go look for them.

> **年幼的孩子：**他们都在这座游乐园里，爸爸妈妈先进去了。我正要去找他们。

**Young Child:** I have to go, goodbye Mister. Hope you have a good time too!

> **年幼的孩子：**所以我得走啦，再见，先生。祝你也能玩得开心！

[Recap] Aventurine's "future self" proclaimed that Aventurine had set up a grand betrayal, and Dr. Ratio leaking the plan was a part of it. He questioned Aventurine, why did he reject Elation and choose Preservation?

*(Approach the curtains)*

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**???:** Only on Penacony, am I right?

> **？？？：**深不见底，就和匹诺康尼一样，对吧？

**Aventurine:** What are you still doing here?

> **砂金：**你怎么还在？

**???:** You've known all along, if The Family truly showed leniency to everyone who came seeking help, why would there be a need for such high walls and deep moats?

> **？？？：**你早就清楚，如果家族真对每一位前来求援的家人都投以宽容，又何必这样高垒深堑？

**???:** However, people don't see it like that, especially since the flavor of the dream syrup is so alluring. You will receive no charity on Penacony, and relying on your own strength alone to topple high walls...? Not a likely feat.

> **？？？：**但人们不这么想，毕竟美梦糖浆的味道实在诱人。你在匹诺康尼孤立无援，只能凭一己之力扳倒高墙…怎么可能？

**???:** Which is why once you step into the hotel, you remove your high hat and beg everyone you come across for help, like a hyena scavenging for scraps in the desert. Because you know that opportunities are fleeting.

> **？？？：**所以一踏进酒店，你就摘下高高的帽子，开始四处求人，像极了一条在沙漠里捡食的鬣狗。因为你知道，机会稍纵即逝。

**Aventurine:** When you put it like that, even Ratio's "Attini Peacock" analogy sounds pleasant.

> **砂金：**跟你的说法相比，拉帝奥的「阿蒂尼孔雀」都显得动听极了。

**???:** You know how rare it is for me to give you the straight dope, so listen while you can.

> **？？？：**你知道我很少说真心话，劝你把它听进去。

> **？？？：**正好，你提到了那位教授——我特别喜欢你和他的共同点，阴谋和算计…尤其是结局的那部分，一场华丽的背叛！

**???:** ...When everyone thinks this way, who would even suspect that it was another trap you've meticulously devised?

> **？？？：**…当所有人都这么以为的时候，谁又会去怀疑，那是你精心设下的又一场圈套呢？

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**???:** Go on. Tell me I'm right. You know who you really are, Mr. Cavalier Gambler: Uptight, overcautious, massive inferiority complex. You've won so much, and you're still SO afraid of losing.

> **？？？：**我说对了么？你就是这样的人，谨小慎微又妄自菲薄，赢了这么多，却还是比谁都怕输。

**???:** They only see your big bets, your bravado, the full house, the straight flush. They don't know the other hand is below the table, clutching your chips for dear life.

> **？？？：**人们只看见你在牌局上一掷千金，却不知道在牌桌下还有另一只手，握紧筹码，颤抖不已……

**???:** It's a heck of an act. No wonder the tavern sent you an invite. You're a natural, kid. You don't stop at fooling the audience — you fool yourself too.

> **？？？：**厉害啊，难怪酒馆会给你发邀请。你天生就是个好演员…不光擅长骗别人，更擅长骗自己。

**Aventurine:** The best way to prevent others from seeing your true colors, is first being able to fool yourself.

> **砂金：**要想让自己不在人面前露馅，最高明的办法就是先骗过自己。

**???:** Haha, of course, I know you all too well... But, it's strange — why did you decline that invitation? You had the chance to embrace Elation. Was that not what you most wanted? But you chose the IPC instead...

> **？？？：**哈哈，当然，我太了解你了…不过，真奇怪，为什么你拒绝了那份邀请？明明你有过拥抱「欢愉」的机会，那不是你最想要的吗？可你还是选择了公司的牌桌……

**???:** For the Preservation? Hmph, I doubt it. Do you even have anything in common with the Preservation?

> **？？？：**为了「存护」？哼，我看不像。你和「存护」有半点关系吗？

> **砂金：**我以为你知道呢…你不是很懂我么？

> **砂金：**行了，要么现在闭上嘴，要么赶紧从我眼前消失。

> **？？？：**没问题。不过，即将在这里消失的——到底是谁呢？

**Aventurine:** ...It's not going to be me anyway.

> **砂金：**……反正不是我。

Aventurine had to take a risky gamble and deceive The Family to reclaim his power within the Dreamscape.

[Recap] Aventurine picked up a length of iron chain, and memories of his time as a slave surged back like a tide. "His future self" called Aventurine weak for unwilling to cheat and topple the game table, choosing instead to maintain a "fair" game.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Aventurine:** Is this...

> **砂金：**这是……

**Aventurine:** A topaz? What is this doing here...

> **砂金：**「托帕石」？为什么会在这里……

**Aventurine:** Uhh...

> **砂金：**唔……

**???:** What, does her cornerstone wrench at your heart so?

> **？？？：**怎么，她的基石就这么让你撕心裂肺？

**Aventurine:** ...I was merely curious why it was here.

> **砂金：**…我只是好奇它为什么在这里罢了。

**???:** Maybe that winged guy put it here to taunt you. Just to make you realize that your painstakingly arranged magic show is nothing but a death rattle.

> **？？？：**兴许是那个翅膀头为了嘲讽你才故意放这的。好让你明白，你费劲布置的魔术大秀不过是垂死挣扎。

**???:** "The cornerstone's hue is the same as the radiance of Qlipoth's body"... I've got to give it to you, I've heard a lot of baloney, but that lie deserves a prize for sheer nerve. If he were just a little bit smarter, the jig would've been up right there.

> **？？？：**「基石的色泽和克里珀圣体的光芒如出一辙」…亏你能编出这种荒唐的借口。但凡他多长个心眼，你的谎言便一触即溃。

**Aventurine:** This is just bait.

> **砂金：**这只是个诱饵。

**???:** Of course! That's why Ratio's betrayal was one of the keys to your plan. I have to say, that doctor's acting was superb.

> **？？？：**当然！所以你才会把拉帝奥的「背叛」也列为计划中关键的一环。不得不说，那位教授的演技十分逼真。

**Aventurine:** Or maybe he wasn't acting at all?

> **砂金：**也许他根本没有在演呢？

**???:** All the better for you!

> **？？？：**哈，那岂不是更正中你的下怀？

**???:** Sunday didn't become head of the Oak Family by acting sloppy. He's obsessed with control. You have to give him enough detail to satisfy his meticulous nature, but not so much that he gets suspicious.

> **？？？：**那位一丝不苟的橡木家主有着近乎偏执的控制欲，你必须让他掌握足够多的信息，又不能让他察觉到破绽……

**???:** ...Which is why you had Ratio seek him out and leak the plan on purpose — to prevent the other party from suspecting anything. The intel you gave to Ratio was all true, and he spoke the same to Sunday.

> **？？？：**…所以你让拉帝奥找到他，故意把计划泄露出去。为了不让对方起疑，你向拉帝奥交代的内容全都是真实的，他也如实转告了星期日。

**???:** Finally, Sunday took the bait, found the other cornerstone, and before you know it...

> **？？？：**最后，这位家主通过你布下的「诱饵」，如愿以偿找到了另一块基石，如此一来——

**???:** ...Everyone's distracted enough for you to steal the third stone, right out from under them.

> **？？？：**——你才能将第三块石头瞒天过海。

**Aventurine:** ...Why don't you stop rummaging through my mind.

> **砂金：**…别在我脑子里乱翻了，混蛋。

> **？？？：**你的脑子？是我们的脑子。你就是我，我就是你，不分彼此。

**???:** "The best way to prevent others from seeing your true colors, is to first be able to fool yourself"... Really? You can't even fool yourself. You just got lucky this time.

**???:** This is the other cornerstone in Sunday's hands. A rather beautiful green, just like you, smooth and cunning...

> **？？？：**这是星期日手里的另一块基石。相当漂亮的绿色，就像你一样，圆滑、狡诈……

> **？？？：**哼…那我就亲自让你回忆一下。「砂金」是幸运与诡计之石…你拿到这块石头的时候，她是这么说的，对吧？

**???:** This type of stone isn't rare, but its hue is very similar to a certain gem. In fact, it's often used as a substitute. And that more precious gem is...

> **？？？：**这种石头并不珍稀，但色泽却与某种宝石十分接近，因而常被用作后者的替代。而那种更昂贵的宝石……

**Aventurine:** ...Jade.

> **砂金：**…叫做「翡翠」。

**Aventurine:** Even Sunday can't tell the difference. It looks like jade can be substituted for aventurine, too. Sauce for the goose...

> **砂金：**就连那位星期日也没能分辨出来，看来翡翠也并非不能替代砂金。

**???:** Aventurine, Topaz, Jade... Three elites, three cornerstones who, for a measly Penacony, offered their everything. You're even more united than The Family.

> **？？？：**砂金、托帕、翡翠…三位精英，三块基石，为了小小的匹诺康尼，不惜押上自己的全部。你们比家族还团结一心啊。

**Aventurine:** As I said before, three chips are sufficient — all, or nothing.

> **砂金：**我早说过，三枚「筹码」足矣——所有，或者一无所有。

> **砂金：**究竟是前者还是后者？哈，我们很快就能见真章了。

**???:** So, where's the real aventurine stone? Take it out. Let's have a look.

> **？？？：**所以，真正的「砂金石」呢？拿出来看看吧。

**Aventurine:** Heh, suddenly you don't know where it is?

> **砂金：**呵，你又不知道它在哪儿了？

**???:** I just want to hear you say it... After all, it really does resemble its owner.

> **？？？：**我只是要你亲口说出来…毕竟它如今的样子真的和主人很相称。

> **砂金：**…那我就如你所愿。*它们*一直待在最适合的地方，从未离开……

**Aventurine:** Piled up with these cheap baubles.

> **砂金：**…就在这堆廉价的珠宝里。

**???:** You smashed the aventurine stone before you left...?

> **？？？：**你在出发前，就把「砂金石」砸碎了……

**???:** ...Just look at it, tsk. Shattered, just like your life. Poor thing. A humble pebble coated in the most lustrous sheen. I take it back, this thing is far more precious than your life.

> **？？？：**…看它的样子，啧啧，多像你支离破碎的人生啊。再怎么披着光鲜亮丽的外表，里头依旧是颗卑微的小石子…这玩意可比你的命重要得多。

**???:** You're absolutely clear about the consequences of doing this? Blasphemy against Qlipoth's body. You think the IPC will let you get off scot-free?

> **？？？：**这么做的下场你再清楚不过，亵渎克里珀圣体之人，你觉得公司会放过你？

**Aventurine:** Diamond has always been all about results. As long as I can create value far beyond the cost, the ends justify the means.

> **砂金：**「钻石」向来看重结果。只要我能创造的价值远高于成本…过程和手段就不是问题。

**Aventurine:** How else would The Family be fooled if there was no price to pay? It doesn't matter — even smashed to smithereens, the Preservation's cornerstone can still be used. Its effect may be greatly diminished, but... it's enough for me.

> **砂金：**不付出一点代价，怎么能骗过家族？没关系，「存护」的基石就算裂成碎片也能发挥作用，尽管效用会大打折扣，但对我来说…足够了。

**???:** Now I'm really curious. Why does every step you take involve reckless risks, and the choices you prepare for yourself always come with a strong impulse for self-destruction?

> **？？？：**我真的有点好奇了。为什么你迈出的每一步都在铤而走险，为自己准备的选项永远伴随强烈的自毁冲动？

**???:** Do you truly believe that the greater the risk, the greater the rewards? I wouldn't have guessed you'd be so loyal to the IPC.

**Aventurine:** Of course I can. It's just another gamble.

> **砂金：**我当然能做到，不过是又一场赌博而已。

**Aventurine:** I came from the wastelands of Sigonia. For just sixty red copper coins, people paid to brand me, put me in chains, place me in the gallows, and bury me in the golden sands...

> **砂金：**我从茨冈尼亚的荒漠走来，为了六十枚赤铜币，人们在我身上烙下印记，为我戴上枷锁，将我送上刑架，埋入黄沙……

**Aventurine:** But the sun could not kill me, and the quicksand sent me back to the embrace of the Guild and the IPC. Bear in mind, my victory wasn't just a stroke of luck — I've never been defeated.

> **砂金：**可太阳杀不死我，流沙反将我送向学会和公司的怀抱。记住，我不是偶然赢了一次，我从来没有输过。

**Aventurine:** Have you ever heard the saying "Sleep is the rehearsal of death"? Why does life slumber? All because we are not ready for the final rest.

> **砂金：**给各位分享一则谚语吧：「睡眠是死亡的预演」。生命因何而沉睡？因为我们尚未准备好迎接死亡。

**Aventurine:** Every night is practice for the end. You and I are escaping into our dreams for fear of death at this very moment. And "Death"... will surface in our dreams.

> **砂金：**每一夜的入梦与赴死无异，正如此时此刻的你我，心怀死志，躲入睡乡。而「死亡」…也将应我们的梦呓前来。

[Recap] Aventurine awakened to find himself in a black and white world, surrounded by a vast black hole and pitch-black seas. The phantom of young Kakavasha appeared there, like scattered fragments of memory.

**Acheron:** It's a pity this is not the place you were expecting.

[Recap] Acheron reviewed Aventurine's plan. The Family had used the Harmony to construct a barrier isolating the sweet dream, and only through "Death" could one reach the true dream where the Watchmaker's Legacy lay. Aventurine had deliberately positioned himself against Acheron, forcing her to draw her blade.

**Acheron:** ...

> **黄泉：**……

**Aventurine:** Nihility... is it?

> **砂金：**「虚无」…是么？

**Acheron:** Perhaps to you, I am just an Emanator who's hiding her identity, but...

> **黄泉：**也许在你看来，我是一位隐藏身份的「令使」，但是……

**Acheron:** The sleeping and shapeless never glance at anyone. THEY have no face, no form, and even less of a will to speak... The Nihility envelops everyone equally.

> **黄泉：**沉眠无相者从不瞥视任何人，祂无貌无形，更无意志可言…「虚无」平等地笼罩着每个人。

**Acheron:** Only some who have gone under THEIR shadow can go farther, tainting themselves with more Nihility... That's all.

> **黄泉：**只是有些人在祂的阴影下走得更远，沾染了更多的「无」…仅此而已。

**Aventurine:** That's all... My friend, you really leave me at a loss for words.

> **砂金：**仅此而已…朋友，你真的让我不知该怎么接话了。

**Aventurine:** So... Is this my final destination, the land of the dead?

> **砂金：**所以…这就是我的终点，死后之地？

**Acheron:** This is all but a fleeting dream, one of the thousands of manifestations of IX... Under the watchful eye of Nihility, we momentarily linger here, before moving on to our own paths.

> **黄泉：**这只是一场转瞬即逝的梦，「Ⅸ」的万千表征之一…在「虚无」的见证下，我们在此短暂停留，然后行向各自的方向。

**Aventurine:** It seems that my death has already been determined.

> **砂金：**看来我的死亡已经注定。

**Acheron:** Even if you wish for it... I can't promise you anything.

> **黄泉：**即便你希望如此…我也无法给出承诺。

**Acheron:** Now that you've accomplished your goal, I think you can be a little more forthcoming.

> **黄泉：**既然目的已经达成，我想你可以更坦诚些。

**Aventurine:** What do you mean?

> **砂金：**什么意思？

**Acheron:** Your performance at the theme park was wonderful and grandiose... A simple yet practical technique that fooled almost everyone.

> **黄泉：**你在乐园的表演十分精彩，虚张声势…单纯但实用的技巧，骗过了几乎所有人。

**Acheron:** No one would have ever thought that you would have gone to such lengths, even staking your life, just to prove a fact that had seemingly been disproved long ago...

> **黄泉：**不会有人想到，你如此大费周章，甚至不惜押注自己的生命，只是为了再度确认一个看似早已被否定的事实……

**Acheron:** "'Real death' does not exist in Penacony's Dreamscape."

> **黄泉：**「匹诺康尼的梦境中并不存在『真正的死亡』」。

**Aventurine:** ...Why would I do this?

> **砂金：**…我有什么理由这么做？

**Acheron:** Because this is the only way you can uncover a secret that is even more unspeakable than the serial murders...

> **黄泉：**因为只有这样，你才能触及那个比连环凶案更不可告人的秘密……

**Acheron:** To use this "dream death" to get there, to that promised land people constantly seek in this grand gathering...

> **黄泉：**你才能借「梦中的死亡」去往那里，在这场盛会中，人们时刻寻求的那片应许之地……

**Acheron:** Penacony — the Legacy of the Watchmaker, the true land of exile.

> **黄泉：**…钟表匠的遗产，真正的「匹诺康尼」。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** ...How did you find out?

> **砂金：**…你是怎么发现的？

**Acheron:** I never imagined that something I learned about unexpectedly would become the key to connecting everything.

> **黄泉：**我也未曾料想，自己意外得知的某件事，会成为串联一切的关键。

**Aventurine:** It's that person's identity, isn't it?

> **砂金：**是「那个人」的身份，对吧？

**Acheron:** I see you're in the know.

> **黄泉：**看来你也知情。

**Aventurine:** Let's just say I'd put money on the possibility.

> **砂金：**我不能确定，但我愿意赌那个可能性。

**Aventurine:** ...The murder isn't nearly enough to disrupt business as usual. Even if there were one or two murders on Penacony, most people wouldn't be personally affected — and that won't create any waves.

> **砂金：**…「命案」是个好借口，但还远远不够。即便匹诺康尼真的存在那么一两起谋杀，影响的也只是极少数人，掀不起波澜。

**Aventurine:** This dream of theirs isn't a boundless sea, it's a lonely island. The Family used the Harmony to build a high wall and isolate them from the vast and treacherous ocean of the outside world.

> **砂金：**这片美梦并非汪洋大海，而是一座孤岛。家族用「同谐」修筑堤岸高墙，隔绝外界，守护人们不会在大海中溺亡……

**Aventurine:** That barrier they built "keeps death out," but it also keeps the secrets that are lost in that watery abyss from floating to the surface. In a beautiful dream, free of suffering, who would want to go fishing for those secrets. No one. Unless...

> **砂金：**…同时也借助这道「隔绝死亡」的壁垒，将不为人知的秘密埋葬于深海中。在没有痛苦和伤亡的美梦里，那些秘密也会永远不见天日。除非……

**Acheron:** Unless someone goes to the other side of the barrier...

> **黄泉：**除非有人去往壁垒的另一边……

**Acheron:** ...And lives to tell the tale.

> **黄泉：**…并且能活着回来。

**Aventurine:** Someone already has.

> **砂金：**有人已经做到了。

**Aventurine:** I got the idea early on, chewing on that Masked Fool's little hint. If a mute isn't "someone who cannot make a sound," then it has to be "someone who cannot speak."

> **砂金：**我很早就获得了提示：如果哑巴指向的并非「不能发声之人」，那就只可能是「不能说话之人」……

**Aventurine:** Someone who survived the treacherous depths, but is unable to take the stage and speak the truth. I'm happy to know she's safe and sound, and still on Penacony.

> **砂金：**那个已然从深海中生还，却无法再走到台前开口说话的人——我很高兴得知她依旧在匹诺康尼，并且平安无事。

**Acheron:** "Hint" ...is that not "proof"?

> **黄泉：**「提示」…不是「证据」么？

**Aventurine:** Proof is the one thing I don't have. The only thing that can prove these... conjectures... is for The Family to come clean. And with the way they buttered up these outsiders, it seems pretty clear they're intent on covering their tracks.

> **砂金：**很遗憾，我没有证据。唯一能佐证这些猜想的，也只有家族面对「死亡」时的坦诚。他们对外来者太过慷慨，反而显得欲盖弥彰。

**Aventurine:** But you don't need proof to have a suspicion, and for me suspicion is enough. I didn't need to find the memory zone meme. I just needed for someone to kill me like it killed the silver-haired girl.

> **砂金：**但怀疑一件事不需要证据，解开真相才要——对我而言，前者便已足够。我也无需找到那只忆域迷因，只要有人能像它一样「杀死」我即可。

**Acheron:** You don't sound very confident to me. Going out of your way to make citywide broadcasts in an attempt to involve more people... You are simply betting on the possibility of someone being able to "break down the barrier."

> **黄泉：**在我看来，你其实没有十足的把握。特地进行全城广播，试图拉更多人入局…也是因为你在赌一个「有人能打破壁垒」的可能性。

**Acheron:** You're very lucky that fate has decided to let us cross paths. I happen to be equipped with a very sharp blade — sharp enough to slice through the veil of dreams. I can also carve the Harmony's brand off of you...

> **黄泉：**你确实很幸运，命运使我们的道路交汇，而我恰好配有一柄利刃——锋利到足以斩落美梦的帷幕，同时将你身上「同谐」的烙印一刀两断……

**Acheron:** You possess great cunning. Deliberately setting us up to be on opposing sides, constantly repeating the words of the Emanator in front of others, leaving me no choice but to draw my blade against you.

> **黄泉：**你也很狡猾，故意设计让我们站在彼此的对立面，不断在他人面前重复「令使」的说辞，令我退无可退，唯有拔刀相向。

**Acheron:** And that's how you win. Opportunity and strategy, both are essential.

> **黄泉：**所以你才能赢。时运和谋略，缺一不可。

**Acheron:** And in your plans, the IPC always wins. Even if you lose the bet... To The Family, the life of an ambassador is still invaluable.

> **黄泉：**而在你的布局里，公司永远是赢家，即便最后你赌输了…对于家族而言，一位使节的性命也足够昂贵。

**Aventurine:** It's a huge gamble, isn't it? But, allow me to point out a mistake: The IPC's success is not guaranteed — I unfortunately have no contingencies for such an important matter.

> **砂金：**一场豪赌，不是么？但容我指出一个错误：公司并非稳操胜券，在一件至关重要的事上，我的确没有后手。

**Aventurine:** Detonating a Stellaron... I can't do it. The aventurine stone is too broken to even safeguard my escape from the stage.

> **砂金：**引爆一颗星核…我做不到。「砂金石」已经太过破碎，甚至无法保护我从舞台上全身而退。

**Aventurine:** If at the end of the day you did not unsheathe your blade... I would have lost the bet.

> **砂金：**如果你到最后都没有拔出那把刀…就是我满盘皆输了。

**Acheron:** It is pointless to discuss "what-ifs." You have won. Your prize is an entry ticket into that deep sea.

> **黄泉：**讨论「如果」没有意义。是你赢了，你为自己赢得了通往那片深海的入场券。

**Acheron:** And after this, whether you can return from the abyss... is another gamble of yours.

> **黄泉：**而这之后，能否从深渊中归来…就是你的另一场豪赌了。

**Acheron:** ...Have you never wavered?

> **黄泉：**…你不曾犹豫过吗？

**Aventurine:** Wavered? Of course I have. But I can only bank on my own good fortune.

> **砂金：**犹豫…当然。但我只能相信我的好运。

**Aventurine:** Because other than that, I have nothing.

> **砂金：**因为除此之外，我一无所有。

[Recap] Aventurine questioned the meaning of life, and Acheron replied that death was not the end. The meaning of death would change depending on the choices made. Young Kakavasha bid Aventurine farewell, and for the final time, Aventurine proposed a "palm clap."

**Acheron:** ...Wake up from this dream and go to where you should be. Your gamble... is not over yet.

> **黄泉：**…从这场梦中醒来，去你应去的地方吧。你的赌局…尚未结束。

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** ...Before we part, can you answer one more question? As someone who has traveled on that road, can you tell me...

> **砂金：**…在分别前，能再回答我一个问题么？身为走在那条路上的人，你能否告诉我……

**Aventurine:** Why are we born into this world if it's just to die?

> **砂金：**为什么我们要为了死亡而出生在这世上？

**Acheron:** I don't think this, and never have. Nor do you.

> **黄泉：**我从不这么认为。你也一样。

**Aventurine:** But the Nihility envelops you and I... and everyone.

> **砂金：**可「虚无」的确笼罩着你我…还有每一个人。

**Acheron:** And because of that, it's pointless.

> **黄泉：**也正因如此，它没有意义。

**Aventurine:** But it is still there.

> **砂金：**——但它仍在那里。

**Aventurine:** If the dice of fate are always weighted, then that is our destiny. Why then... do we struggle against it?

> **砂金：**倘若命运的骰子从来都被灌铅，那就是我们命定的归宿，我们…又为何要与之相抗？

**Acheron:** ...My answer might not be able to resolve your confusion, because it has been with you throughout your journey, and is already a part of your life.

> **黄泉：**…我的回答未必能消解你的困惑，因为它伴你一路走来，早已是你生命的一部分。

**Acheron:** But you said, "Sleep is the rehearsal of death," so why does life slumber? Because we are not ready for the final rest.

> **黄泉：**但你说过，「睡眠是死亡的预演」，生命因何而沉睡？因为我们尚未准备好迎接死亡。

**Acheron:** So you can definitely understand, why we want to be prepared.

> **黄泉：**所以你也一定能明白，我们为何*想要*做好准备。

**Acheron:** Even if the ending has been predetermined, that's fine. There are countless things that humans cannot change.

> **黄泉：**就算结局早已注定，那也无妨，人改变不了的事太多。

**Acheron:** But before the end, there are many things that humans can do while on their journey.

> **黄泉：**但在此之前，在走向结局的路上，人能做的事同样很多。

**Acheron:** And because of this, the "end" will thus reveal a completely different meaning.

> **黄泉：**而「结局」…也会因此展现截然不同的意义。

**Acheron:** Take a good look at your pocket. Your friend has already given you the answer.

> **黄泉：**看看你的口袋吧，你的朋友早就把答案交给你了。

**Acheron:** ...Good luck.

> **黄泉：**…祝你好运。

*(Quick Read)*

Doctor's Advice

The impossible in the Dreamscape is not "Death," but rather "Dormancy." Do stay alive. I wish you the best of luck.

**Aventurine:** ...

> **砂金：**……

**Aventurine:** ...Then I shall get going.

> **砂金：**…那我也该走了。

**"Kakavasha":** Mister...

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**先生……

**"Kakavasha":** You're leaving? You ultimately chose to... leave this Dreamscape?

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**你要走了吗，你最后还是选择…离开这座梦境？

**Aventurine:** ...Yes. Because they are not here... My papa, mama, and big sis...

> **砂金：**…对。因为他们不在这里…爸爸、妈妈、姐姐……

**"Kakavasha":** Then where are they?

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**那他们在哪儿？

**Aventurine:** They are in a place where everyone will go. A very, very distant place.

> **砂金：**他们在每个人都会去往的地方，一个很远、很远的地方。

**"Kakavasha":** Then are you going too?

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**你也要到那里去？

**Aventurine:** I'll get there, one day.

> **砂金：**我总有一天也会走到那里。

**Aventurine:** But not now.

> **砂金：**但不是现在。

**Aventurine:** There will come a day when the sky will drizzle, and I will hear the call of Gaiathra Triclops and know that it is time for me to go and be reunited with my family.

> **砂金：**会有那么一天，当天上再度洒落下雨，我会听见母神的呼唤，知道命定的时刻已至，我应去和我的家人重逢。

**Aventurine:** So until the time comes... I should be preparing.

> **砂金：**所以在那一刻到来前…我应当做好「准备」。

**"Kakavasha":** Preparing for what?

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**准备好什么？

**Aventurine:** Preparing to face them, Kakavasha, and to make them proud.

> **砂金：**准备好面对他们，卡卡瓦夏，成为他们的骄傲。

**"Kakavasha":** ...

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**……

**"Kakavasha":** I know you'll be able to do it. Good luck!

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**我想，你会做到的。加油。

**Aventurine:** Of course. For I am a child who received the blessing of Gaiathra Triclops.

> **砂金：**当然。因为我是受母神赐福的孩子。

**"Kakavasha":** But you still seem nervous...

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**但你看起来还是很紧张……

**Aventurine:** I seem that way because I am nervous. You know what? Maybe you can help...

> **砂金：**我也这么觉得，也许只有你能帮我个忙了……

**Aventurine:** What do you say? One last time? Put our palms together?

> **砂金：**…最后一次，我们来「对掌」吧？

[Recap] Before departure, Aventurine bid farewell to his past self, sharing one last high-five with little Kakavasha. "Farewell, Kakavasha."

*(Cutscene begins)*

**"Kakavasha":** Are you going now?

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**你要出发了吗？

**Aventurine:** Yes.

> **砂金：**嗯。

**Aventurine:** "May the Mother Goddess thrice close her eyes for you..."

> **砂金：**「愿母神三度为你阖眼……」

**Aventurine and "Kakavasha":** ...keeping your blood eternally pulsing."

> **砂金&「卡卡瓦夏」：**「令你的血脉永远鼓动……」

**Aventurine and "Kakavasha":** "May your journey be forever peaceful..."

> **砂金&「卡卡瓦夏」：**「旅途永远坦然……」

**Aventurine and "Kakavasha":** "...and your schemes forever concealed."

> **砂金&「卡卡瓦夏」：**「…诡计永不败露。」

**"Kakavasha":** Our paths will cross again beneath Kakava's shimmering auroras.

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**我们将在「卡卡瓦」的极光下重逢。

**"Kakavasha":** Farewell, Kakavasha.

> **「卡卡瓦夏」：**再见，卡卡瓦夏。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] In the bottomless dream, the dice fell in silence. The glow of the "Aventurine" faded, signifying that Aventurine had fulfilled his planned objective. Jade and Topaz were set to carry out the remaining duties and begin the "harvest."

"With hope for the morrow nestled in my heart, I descend into the slumber of tonight. Until the denouement of all coming morrows kisses me, I have then embraced the quiet death."

But this man is different. He lives and breathes in the present. In every sinking night, by every daring gamble, no vision of morrow ever graced his dreams.

His life knows not quietude. His fate yet demands him to win them all, to weather tempests, one after another... til mire enshrouds his very breath.

And now, in the unfathomable depths of dream, the once falling die... has at last landed on its earthly rest.

Quietly, peacefully, it at last landed.

...

**Topaz:** The light of the aventurine stone... has disappeared.

> **托帕：**「砂金石」的光芒…消失了。

**Topaz:** This only represents one outcome...

> **托帕：**这只代表一种结果……

**Jade:** He kept his promise...

> **翡翠：**他兑现了承诺。

**Jade:** ...And got what he wanted.

> **翡翠：**…也得偿所愿。

**Topaz:** ...

> **托帕：**……

**Topaz:** As planned, your cornerstone has been successfully sent to The Family's territory. Then—

> **托帕：**按照计划，你的「基石」已被顺利送入家族的领地中。那么——

**Jade:**—Let's fulfill our duty and start "harvesting."

> **翡翠：**——履行我们的职责，开始「收获」吧。

Thereafter, in the cradle of slumberers, deep within the sweet dream of the Planet of Festivities —

Another stone begins to radiate light.

**Jade:** "I come to greet. I come to wine. I come to claim."

> **翡翠：**「我来觐见、我来添酒、我来占有。」

**Jade:** "I grant poison for nectar. Sow in spring to reap in fall. Await wizened fruit to fill livened boughs."

> **翡翠：**「我为甘露赐下鸩毒，春种秋收，静待枯果满枝头。」

**Jade:** "All for the... Amber Lord."

> **翡翠：**「一切献给…琥珀王。」

Meanwhile — Memory Zone Depths

...

You try to open your eyes, but find only blackness before you.

- ▸ Where is this?
- ▸ What happened?
- ▸ And whose POV is this?

Memories gradually resurface as time rewinds to a few minutes ago: Aventurine unleashes the final assault, pouring down a dazzling shower of chips, followed closely by Acheron drawing her blade, and then, *crash*—

The indescribable force severs the power of Preservation, while time and space froze instantly. Your thoughts twist into a knot as your senses fail, with only gravity tearing at your mind as you plummet into boundless darkness...

...until a fire engulfs you in its embrace.

- ▸ Yes. I'm (Trailblazer).
- ▸ I think someone saved me...
- ▸ Doesn't matter. I need to get out of here.

[Recap] Your thoughts plunged into endless darkness until a flicker of firelight embraced you. You saw SAM, who seemed as if they had something to say.

**Sam:** ...You're awake.

> **萨姆：**…你醒了。

**Sam:** I've been waiting on you for quite a while.

> **萨姆：**我在这里等你很久了。

- ▸ And you are...?

**Sam:** You've met me before — I'm Sam — the Stellaron Hunter.

> **萨姆：**你已见过我了，星核猎手萨姆。

- ▸ Just what's happening here?

**Sam:** A crevice between dreams. You've met me before — I'm Sam — a Stellaron Hunter.

> **萨姆：**梦与梦之间的狭缝。你已见过我了，星核猎手萨姆。

- ▸ It's you... what have you done?

**Sam:** I didn't do anything but wait for you to wake up. You've met me before, I'm Sam — a Stellaron Hunter.

> **萨姆：**我什么都没有做，只是等你醒来。你已见过我了，星核猎手萨姆。

**Sam:** I originally planned on showing up earlier to reveal some truths to you.

> **萨姆：**我本想更早出现在你面前，向你道出一些事实。

**Sam:** But I encountered more roadblocks than expected. Eleven times, I've tried, but ended in failure. Before I knew it, this world and I became too intertwined and it became too difficult to escape the constraints of the "script."

> **萨姆：**但我受到的阻拦比预想更甚。11次，我做出尝试，却以失败告结，不知不觉中，我与这世界的联系变得太过紧密，难以逃离「剧本」的约束。

**Sam:** ...Elio is right. In this Land of the Dreams, you and I will reap unforgettable gains.

> **萨姆：**…艾利欧说的没错，在这片梦想之地，你我都会得到难忘的收获。

**Sam:** I don't know people's hearts as well as he and Kafka do, nor do I have a specialty like Silver Wolf and Blade. Most of the things that I'm good at only apply to villains who need no mercy.

> **萨姆：**我不如他和卡芙卡那样通晓人心，也没有银狼和刃的一技之长。我所擅长的种种，大多也只适用于无需怜悯的恶徒。

**Sam:** So, there is only one method that I use.

> **萨姆：**所以——我所能使用的「手段」也只有一种。

[Recap] Sam deactivated the mech form, revealing Firefly within the fire. The only "method" Firefly had left was to show you everything she's got.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Sam:** This is to show to you...

> **萨姆：**那就是向你展示…

**Firefly:** ...all that I am.

> **流萤：**我的全部。

*(Cutscene ends)*

[Recap] Sunday revealed that Gallagher was in fact a servant of the Enigmata, his existence fabricated from the traits of fifty-two members of The Family. He accused Gallagher of manipulating Memory Zone Meme to murder his sister and demanded an explanation.

**Gallagher:** Labyrinth-like corridors and halls, traps everywhere... The owner of this mansion must be a bit paranoid.

> **加拉赫：**迷宫一样的走廊和厅房，无处不在的陷阱机关…这大宅子的主人疑心病有点重啊。

**Sunday:** You're so funny, Mr. Security Officer. I hope that sense of humor of yours has helped you find the serial killer.

> **星期日：**你很幽默，治安官先生。希望这份幽默感已经帮助你找到了连环杀人案的凶手。

**Gallagher:** Just expressing a personal opinion. Why, did I hit a nerve?

> **加拉赫：**只是发表一下个人看法。怎么，戳到你痛处了？

**Sunday:** Mr. Gallagher, my patience is wearing thin. Neglecting duties...

> **星期日：**加拉赫先生，我的耐心不多。消极怠工——

**Sunday:** ...will only make me more suspicious that you and the real serial killer are connected.

> **星期日：**——只会让我更加怀疑你与真凶有所牵连。

**Gallagher:** ...

> **加拉赫：**……

**Gallagher:** Scoundrel, punk, drunk, hooligan... I've heard this trash talk all too often. But I have never once thought that I'd be treated as an accomplice to a murderer!

> **加拉赫：**无赖、混混、酒鬼、流氓…这些垃圾话我可听过太多，但我真没想到，自己有朝一日还能被当作杀人魔的共犯。

**Gallagher:** I take back what I said: Your problem isn't paranoia. You're just crazy, you know? Lunatic.

> **加拉赫：**我收回前言：你的问题不是疑心太重。你是个疯子，懂吗？疯子。

**Gallagher:** You — The Family — you broke my spine and pulled out my fangs, and now you want to accuse me of murder? Ridiculous, only idiots who've drunk too much SoulGlad will berate a stray dog on the streets.

> **加拉赫：**你们——家族——把我这条老狗的脊梁骨打断，拔了獠牙，现在又开始指控我杀人？混账，只有苏乐达喝多了的白痴才会对街边的流浪狗发神经。

**Gallagher:** What exactly is making you say all this nonsense? You should be more concerned about the outworld visitors who are making a scene in the theme park than me!

> **加拉赫：**究竟是什么东西让你在这不停地说疯话？比起我，你更应该去关心那群正在影视乐园闹得热火朝天的外宾。

**Sunday:** I don't need you to remind me. The moment that ambassador walked out the doors of the mansion, I knew what he was trying to do. My servants see everything. His little magic tricks may have fooled me, but no matter, I'm happy to see how things have turned out.

> **星期日：**用不着你提醒。那位使节一出公馆的门，我就明白他想干什么，我的*仆人*隐夜鸫全都看在眼里。他的小魔术确实骗过了我，但无妨，我非常乐意看见现在的局面。

**Sunday:** Why do you think that I just let him go? And why do you think I emptied the theme park stage?

> **星期日：**你以为我为什么会放他走，又是为了什么才把那座影视乐园的舞台专门空出来？

**Sunday:** Because my target from the beginning has always been you, Hound. The more noise he makes, the more opportunities I have to make you and your true master pay in blood!

> **星期日：**因为我的目标从始至终就是你，猎狗。他闹出的动静越大，我就越有机会让你和你真正的主人血债血偿。

**Gallagher:** If I were really the murderer, why would you need to be so secretive? Ha, I forgot, you also have a difficult master to serve — telling you to ignore the murder case and focus solely on that Charmony Festival...

> **加拉赫：**如果我真是凶手，你又何必这么遮遮掩掩？哈，我忘了，你也有个不好伺候的主子呢——他们叫你别管什么狗屁凶杀案，专心搞那「谐乐大典」……

**Gallagher:** ...Isn't that right, my brother?

> **加拉赫：**…是不是啊，*温柔的兄长*？

**Sunday:** ...Looks like your disguise has helped you successfully understand every facet of The Family.

> **星期日：**…看来你的伪装已经帮你充分了解到家族的每一处细节了。

**Gallagher:** Disguise? You must be blind to be accusing me of being a fake. Open your eyes and take a good look...

> **加拉赫：**伪装？你哪只眼睛看出来我是假人了？睁大眼睛仔细瞧瞧吧，带光环的——

**Sunday:** ...

> **星期日：**……

**Sunday:** Indeed, every part of you is real. The brown hair, soft and curly like Benny's; the orange eyes, which make me miss the gaze of Sir Whittaker; that odd scar, the mark of Woolsey...

> **星期日：**诚然，你身上的每一处都是真实的。棕色的头发，像班尼一样柔软、卷曲；橙色的眼珠，令我怀念惠特克爵士的视线；古怪的伤疤，它是伍尔西的勋章……

**Sunday:** And the gray vest, tie, Hound Emblem, bottle, the bartending and your role as a security officer... These are all true traits...

> **星期日：**还有灰马甲、领带、猎犬勋章、水壶、调饮技术、治安官的身份…它们全都是真实的——

**Sunday:** ...from all fifty-two loyal Family members.

> **星期日：**——来自五十二位忠诚的*家族成员*。

**Sunday:** When they are gathered, countless tiny truths are woven together into a lie — you collected a small piece of each of them and claimed them for yourself. Then you invented this facade, a complete Gallagher...

> **星期日：**当它们汇聚于一处时，无数细小的真实便编织成谎言——你从每个人身上采撷一缕认知，将它们据为己有，在梦境中*虚构*出了一个完整的「加拉赫」……

**Sunday:** ...Tell me I'm wrong, minion of the Enigmata.

> **星期日：**…我说的对吗，「神秘」的爪牙？

**Gallagher:** ...

> **加拉赫：**……

**Gallagher:** Haha...

> **加拉赫：**哈哈……

**Gallagher:** Hahahaha! You have guts. I'll give you that! Not bad. I severely underestimated you...

> **加拉赫：**哈哈哈哈哈哈！你有种，厉害！可以啊，是我太低估你了……

**Gallagher:** Admirable. But so what? Can this prove that I murdered your sister and that stowaway?

> **加拉赫：**我欣赏你。但所以呢？这就能证明是我杀了你的妹妹和那位偷渡犯吗？

**Sunday:** This proves that you and the Memory Zone Meme "Death" are linked — and that's enough.

> **星期日：**这能证明你和忆域迷因「死亡」是一丘之貉——已经足够了。

**Sunday:** Listen up. I don't care how you did it... I only care about one thing, the answer to a question...

> **星期日：**听好了，我根本不在乎你是如何做到的…我现在只在乎一件事，一个问题的答案——

**Sunday:** ...You devil! You wretched, despicable dog, why did you kill her!?

> **星期日：**——你这个混账，该死的丧家犬，为什么要杀了她？！

[Recap] Gallagher referred to Sunday as being "too in the thick of things," offering to provide Sunday with answers about Robin's death. Then, the Memory Zone Meme "Death" pierced through Sunday's chest.

*(Cutscene begins)*

**Gallagher:** You know, in the thick of things, people are blind to the grit in their eyes...

> **加拉赫：**当局者迷——人们看不见眼中的沙子，

**Gallagher:** ...yet they can always feel its scratch.

> **加拉赫：**只知道沙子就在那里。

**Gallagher:** Want the answer?

> **加拉赫：**想要答案？

**Gallagher:** I'll give it to you.

> **加拉赫：**我可以给你。

**Gallagher:** The whole thing is just fate playing a cruel joke on us.

> **加拉赫：**…一切只因那该死的天意弄人。

*(Cutscene ends)*

*(Mission completed)*

(Obtain Flames Afar, Kakavasha, and "Citizen Hanunue")
